[page 384]
  Tattha appaṭivānī 1- sudāhaṃ bhikkhave padahāmi
      kāmaṃ taco nahārū ca aṭṭhi ca avasissatu
      avassussatu me sarīre sabbantaṃ maṃsalohitaṃ 2-
  yantaṃ purisatthāmena purisaviriyena purisaparakkamena pattabbaṃ na taṃ
  apāpuṇitvā viriyassa saṇṭhānaṃ bhavissatīti. Tassa mayhaṃ bhikkhave
  appamādādhigatā bodhi appamādādhigato anuttaro yogakkhemo
  tumhe cepi bhikkhave appaṭivānī padaheyyātha kāmaṃ taco nahārū
  ca ... Viriyassa saṇṭhānaṃ bhavissatīti tumhe bhikkhave nacirasseva
  yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadeva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti
  tadanuttaraṃ brahmacariyapariyosānaṃ diṭṭheva dhamme sayaṃ abhiññā
  sacchikatvā upasampajja viharissatha. Tasmātiha bhikkhave evaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ
  appaṭivānī padahissāma kāmaṃ taco nahārū ca ... Viriyassa
  saṇṭhānaṃ bhavissatīti evañhi vo bhikkhave sikkhitabbanti.
                    Caturaṅgaviriyasuttapāli.
      [488] Appaṭivānī sudāhaṃ bhikkhave padahāmīti ettha sudanti
  nipātamattaṃ. Ahaṃ bhikkhave anossakkanāya ṭhito bodhisattakāle
  sabbaññutaṃ paṭṭhento padhānamakāsinti ayamettha attho.
  Idāni yathānena taṃ padhānaṃ kataṃ taṃ dassento kāmaṃ
  tacotiādimāha. Tattha yantanti iminā pattabbaṃ sabbaṃ guṇajātaṃ
  dasseti. Purisatthāmenātiādīsu purisassa ñāṇatthāmo ñāṇaviriyaṃ

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. appaṭivāṇaṃ. 2. sarīre upasussatu maṃsalohitanti pāliyaṃ dissati.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 385]
  Ñāṇaparakkamo ca kathito. Saṇṭhānanti ṭhapanā appanā appavattanā
  ossakkanā paṭippassaddhīti attho. Ettāvatānena
  caturaṅgasamannāgataṃ viriyādhiṭṭhānaṃ nāma kathitaṃ. Ettha hi kāmaṃ tacoti ekaṃ
  aṅgaṃ nahārū cāti ekaṃ aṭṭhi cāti ekaṃ maṃsalohitanti ekaṃ imāni
  cattāri aṅgāni. Purisatthāmenātiādīni adhimattaviriyādhivacanāni.
  Iti purimehi catūhi aṅgehi samannāgataṃ hutvā evaṃ adhiṭṭhitaṃ viriyaṃ
  caturaṅgasamannāgataṃ viriyādhiṭṭhānaṃ nāmāti veditabbaṃ. Ettāvatānena
  bodhipallaṅke attano āgamanīyappaṭipadā kathitā. Idāni tāya
  paṭipadāya paṭiladdhaguṇaṃ kathetuṃ tassa mayhaṃ bhikkhavetiādimāha. Tattha
  appamādādhigatāti satiavippavāsasaṅkhātena appamādena adhigatā
  na suttappamattena laddhā. Bodhīti catummaggaññāṇañceva
  sabbaññutaññāṇañca. Na hi sakkā etaṃ suttappamattena
  adhigantunti. Tenāha appamādādhigatā bodhīti. Anuttaro
  yogakkhemoti na kevalaṃ bodhiyeva arahattaphalanibbānasaṅkhāto
  anuttaro yogakkhemopi appamādādhigatova. Idāni attanā
  paṭiladdhaguṇesu bhikkhusaṅghaṃ samādapento tumhe cepi
  bhikkhavetiādimāha. Tattha yassatthāyāti yassa atthāya yaṃ upasampajja
  viharitukāmā hutvāti attho. Tadanuttaranti taṃ anuttaraṃ.
  Brahmacariyapariyosānanti maggabrahmacariyassa pariyosāne bhūtaṃ
  ariyaphalaṃ. Abhiññā sacchikatvāti abhiññāya uttamappaññāya
  paccakkhaṃ katvā. Upasampajjāti paṭilabhitvā pāpuṇitvā
-------------------------------------------------
[page 386]
  Viharissatha. Tasmāti yasmā appaṭivānappadhānaṃ nāmetaṃ bahūpakāraṃ
  uttamatthasādhakaṃ tasmāti tabbaṇṇanā 1-
      [489] Viriyappavāhe vattamāne antarāeva paṭigamanaṃ
  nivattanaṃ paṭivānaṃ. Tadassa atthīti paṭivānī. Na paṭivānī
  appaṭivānī. Āgamanīyappaṭipadāti samathavipassanāsaṅkhātā
  pubbabhāgappaṭipatti. Sā hi āgacchanti visesamadhigacchanti etāya
  āgacchati vā visesādhigamo etāyāti āgamanīyā. Sāeva
  paṭipajjitabbato paṭipadāti āgamanīyappaṭipadā. Appaṭivānappadhānanti
  ossakkanārahitappadhānaṃ antarā anossakkitvā kataviriyanti
  atthoti taṭṭīkā.
      [490] Tasmā kenaci viriyaṃ na ossajjitabbaṃ. Yadi tādisaṃ
  paccayamāgamma pubbe ossajjitaṃ pacchā sampaggaṇhitabbameva
  ossaṭṭhaviriyabhikkhunā viya.
      So kira satthu santike kammaṭṭhānaṃ gahetvā araññaṃ pavisitvā
  temāsaṃ vāyamantopi kiñci visesaṃ uppādetuṃ asakkonto ye
  te satthārā cattāro puggalā kathitā tesu mayā padaparamena
  bhavitabbaṃ natthi maññe mayhaṃ imasmiṃ attabhāve maggaphalaṃ kiṃ
  araññavāsena satthu santikaṃ gantvā buddharūpaṃ olokento
  madhuradhammaṃ suṇanto viharissāmīti cintetvā jetavanaṃ paccāgañchi.
  Atha naṃ bhikkhū taṃ kāraṇaṃ ñatvā satthu santikaṃ netvā bhante

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. mano. pū. 2/7.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 387]
  Ayaṃ viriyaṃ ossajjitvā āgatoti āhaṃsu. Satthā taṃ upatthambhento
  kiṃ tvaṃ bhikkhu evarūpe niyyānike sāsane pabbajitvā appicchoti
  vā santuṭṭhoti vā pavivittoti vā asaṃsaṭṭhoti vā āraddhaviriyoti
  vā evaṃ attānaṃ ajānāpetvā ossaṭṭhaviriyabhikkhūti
  jānāpesi nanu tvaṃ pubbe viriyavā ahosi tayā ekena kataṃ viriyaṃ
  nissāya marukantāre bahū janā pānīyaṃ labhitvā sukhitā jātā idāni
  kasmā viriyaṃ ossajjesīti āha. So bhikkhu ettakena upatthambhito
  ahosi. Atha satthā bhikkhūhi puṭṭho atītaṃ āhari
      [491] Atīte bārāṇasiyaṃ bodhisatto satthavāhajeṭṭhako hutvā
  pañcahi sakaṭasatehi vāṇijakammaṃ katvā vicaranto ekaṃ saṭṭhiyojanikaṃ
  marukantāraṃ pāpuṇi. Tasmiṃ pana kantāre sukhumā vālukā muṭṭhinā
  gahitā hatthe na tiṭṭhanti suriyuggamanato paṭṭhāya ca aṅgārarāsi
  viya uṇhā honti na sakkā akkamituṃ. Tasmā taṃ paṭipajjantā
  dārūdakatelataṇḍulādīni sakaṭehi ādāya rattimeva gantvā
  aruṇuggamane sakaṭaparivattakaṃ katvā matthake maṇḍapaṃ katvā nisinnā
  aṭṭhaṅgate suriye sāyamāsaṃ bhuñjitvā bhūmiyā sītalajātāya gacchanti.
  Bodhisattopi imināva niyāmena taṃ kantāraṃ gacchanto ekūnasaṭṭhi
  yojanāni gantvā idāni ekaratteneva marukantārā nikkhamanaṃ
  bhavissatīti sāyamāsaṃ bhuñjitvā sabbaṃ udakaṃ khepetvā pāyāsi.
  Niyyāmako purimasakaṭe nipanno ākāse tārakaṃ olokento
  ito goṇe pājetha eto goṇe pājethāti vadamāno
-------------------------------------------------
[page 388]
  Dīghamaddhānaṃ niddābhāvena kilanto niddaṃ okkamitvā goṇe nivattitvā
  āgatamaggameva gaṇhante nāññāsi. Goṇā sabbarattiṃ agamaṃsu.
  Niyyāmako aruṇuggamanavelāya pabuddho tamatthaṃ ñatvā sakaṭāni
  nivattethāti āha. Te sabbe hiyyo nivutthaṃ ṭhānaṃ patvā
  udakampi no khīṇaṃ idāni vinaṭṭhamhāti anusocantā attano
  sakaṭassa heṭṭhā nipajjiṃsu. Bodhisatto pāto sītalavelāyameva
  āhiṇḍanto ekaṃ dabbatiṇagacchaṃ disvā imāni tiṇāni heṭṭhā
  udakasītalena uṭṭhitāni bhavissantīti cintetvā taṃ padesaṃ khanāpesi.
  Saṭṭhihatthaṭṭhānaṃ khanitvā paharantānaṃ heṭṭhā pāsāṇo 1- paṭihaññi.
  Sabbe viriyaṃ ossajjiṃsu. Bodhisatto imassa pāsāṇassa heṭṭhā
  udakena bhavitabbanti okkamitvā sotaṃ odahitvā udakasaddaṃ
  sutvā uttaritvā tāta tayā viriye ossaṭṭhe sabbe vinassissāma
  imaṃ ayakūṭaṃ gahetvā otaritvā pāsāṇaṃ paharāti cūḷupaṭṭhākaṃ
  āṇāpesi. So tathā akāsi. Pāsāṇo dvidhā bhijjitvā
  sotaṃ sannirumhi. Tālakkhandhappamāṇā udakavaṭṭi uggañchi.
  Sabbepi pānīyaṃ pivitvā yathicchakaṃ agamaṃsu.
      Evaṃ satthā atītaṃ āharitvā bhikkhū ovadanto ekanipātassa
  dutiyavagge 2- vaṇṇapathajātake 3- imaṃ gāthamāha

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. pāsāṇe. 2. paṭhamavagge dutiyeti yujjati.
  3. khu. jā. 27/1 tadaṭṭhakathā. 1/169.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 389]
      [492] Akilāsuno vaṇṇapathe khanantā
            udaṅgaṇe tattha papaṃ avinduṃ
            evaṃ muni viriyabalūpapanno
            akilāsu vinde hadayassa santinti.
      Tattha akilāsunoti nikkosajjā āraddhaviriyā. Vaṇṇapatheti
  vaṇṇaṃ vuccati vālukā vālukāmaggeti attho. Khanantāti bhūmiṃ
  khanamānā. Udaṅgaṇeti ettha uda iti nipāto aṅgaṇe
  manussānaṃ sañcaraṇaṭṭhāne bhūmibhāgeti attho. Tatthāti tasmiṃ
  vaṇṇapathe. Papanti udakaṃ. Udakañhi pivanabhāvena papanti vuccati.
  Pavattaṃ vā āpaṃ mahodakanti attho. Avindunti labhiṃsu. Evanti
  opammappaṭipādanaṃ. Munīti moneyyasaṅkhātāya paññāya samannāgato
  puggalo. Viriyabalūpapannoti viriyena ceva kāyabalaññāṇabalena ca
  samannāgato. Akilāsūti nikkosajjo.
      Kāmaṃ taco nahārū ca aṭṭhi ca avasissatu
      avassussatu me sarīre sabbantaṃ maṃsalohitanti
  evaṃ vuttacaturaṅgasamannāgatena viriyena samannāgatattā akilāsu.
  Vinde hadayassa santinti cittassapi hadayarūpassapi sītalabhāvakaraṇena
  santīti saṅkhagataṃ jhānavipassanābhiññāarahattamaggaññāṇasaṅkhātaṃ
  ariyadhammaṃ vindati paṭilabhatīti attho.
      Evañca pana vatvā saccāni pakāsesi. Saccapariyosāne
  so bhikkhu satthārā upatthambhito viriyaṃ sampaggahetvā vipassanaṃ
-------------------------------------------------
[page 390]
  Vaḍḍhetvā arahattaṃ pāpuṇi.
                    Ossaṭṭhaviriyabhikkhuvatthu.
     Ekanipātassa tatiyavagge bhojājānīyajātakaṃ 1- ājaññajātakañca 2-
  tasseva chaṭṭhavagge mahāsīlavajātakañca 3- kathetabbaṃ.
                        Viriyakathā.
     Evaṃ viriyaindriyasaṅkhāto tapo abhijjhādippahānajjhānādippaṭilābhahetuto
  maṅgalanti veditabbo.
                       Tapotipadakathā.

  3. khu. jā. 27/17. tadaṭṭhakathā. 2/41.

[page 391]
                       Brahmacariyakathā
      [493] Brahmacariyaṃ nāma dānaveyyāvaccapañcasīlāppamaññā-
  methunaviratisadārasantosaviriyauposathaṅgāriyamaggasāsanavasena dasavidhaṃ
  hoti. Tathā hi
      [494] Ahañca bhariyā ca manussaloke
            saddhā ubho dānapatī ahumhā ...
            Acchādanaṃ sayanaṃ annapānaṃ
            sakkacca dānāni adamha tattha.
            Taṃ me vataṃ tampana brahmacariyaṃ
            tassa suciṇṇassa ayaṃ vipāko
            iddhī jutī balaviriyūpapatti
            idañca me dhīra mahāvimānanti.
  Vidhūrajātakasaṅkhāte puṇṇakajātake 1- dānaṃ brahmacariyaṃ nāma.
      [495] Tattha manussaloketi aṅgaraṭṭhe kāḷacampakanagare.
  Taṃ me vatanti taṃ sakkaccaṃ dinnadānameva mayhaṃ vattaṃ samādinnañca 2-
  brahmacariyañca ahosi. Tassa ca suciṇṇassa ayaṃ iddhādiko
  vipākoti tabbaṇṇanā 3-.
      Macchariyamalādipāpadhammavigamanato mettādiguṇānubrūhanato ca
  brahmaṃ seṭṭhaṃ cariyanti brahmacariyaṃ dānaṃ. Iddhīti ānubhāvo.
  Jutīti vatthābharaṇobhāsasamujjalā sarīrappabhā. Balaviriyūpapattīti

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. khu. jā. mahā. 28/353. 2. vattasamādānaṃ. 3. jātakaṭṭhakathā. 10/294.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 392]
  Kāyabalena ceva ussāhena ca samannāgatatāti mahāsīhanādasuttaṭīkā.
      Dhīrāti vidhuraṃ ālapati varuṇo nāgarājā.
    [496] Tena pāṇi kāmadado tena pāṇi madhussavo
          tena me brahmacariyena puññaṃ pāṇimhi ijjhatīti
  aṅkurappetavatthumhi veyyāvaccaṃ 1-.
      Imissā gāthāya attho heṭṭhā anavajjakammesu vitthārito
  saddhiṃ vatthunā.
      [497] Idaṃ kho taṃ bhikkhave tittiriyaṃ nāma brahmacariyaṃ ahosīti
  tittirajātake pañcasīlaṃ.
      [498] Taṃ kho pana pañcasikha brahmacariyaṃ na nibbidāya na
  virāgāya na nirodhāya 2- yāvadeva brahmalokūpapattiyāti
  dīghanikāyamahāvagge mahāgovindasutte 3- appamaññāyo.
      [499] Abrahmacariyaṃ pahāya brahmacārīti tikaṅguttare
  uposathasutte 4- brahmajālasuttādīsu 5- ca methunavirati.
      Tattha abrahmacariyanti aseṭṭhacariyaṃ. Brahmaṃ seṭṭhaṃ ācāraṃ
  caratīti brahmacārīti tabbaṇṇanā 6-.
      Aseṭṭhacariyanti aseṭṭhānaṃ nihīnānaṃ aseṭṭhaṃ vā lāmakaṃ nihīnaṃ
  vuttiṃ methunanti attho. Brahmaṃ seṭṭhaṃ ācāranti methunaviratimāhāti
  taṭṭīkā.

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. khu. peta. 26/190. 2. etthantare na upasamāYu..ṇa nibbānāya saṃvattatīti.
  3. dī. mahā. 10/285. 4. aṅ. tika. 20/271. 5. dī. Sī. 9/5.
  6. mano. pū. 2/261.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 393]
      [500] Mayañca bhariyā nātikkamāma
            amhe ca bhariyā nātikkamanti
            aññatra tāhi brahmacariyaṃ carāma
            tasmā hi amhaṃ daharā na miyyareti
  dasakanipāte mahādhammapālajātake 1- sadārasantoso.
      [501] Tattha nātikkamāmāti attano bhariyaṃ atikkamitvā bahi
  micchācāraṃ na karoma. Aññatra tāhīti tā attano bhariyāyo
  ṭhapetvā sesaitthīsu brahmacariyaṃ carāma amhākaṃ bhariyāyopi
  sesapurisesu evameva vattanti. Tasmā hi amhanti ettha hikāro
  nipātamatto. Tena kāraṇena amhākaṃ daharā na miyyanti
  antarā akālamaraṇaṃ nāma natthīti atthoti tabbaṇṇanā 2-.
      Nātikkamāmāti na aticarāma agamanīyaṭṭhānepi itaratthāpi
  na vītikkamāma. Tenāha aññatra tāhīti mahāsīhanādaṭīkā.
      [502] Abhijānāmi kho panāhaṃ sārīputta caturaṅgasamannāgataṃ
  brahmacariyaṃ caritvā 3- tapassī sudaṃ homīti mahāsīhanādasuttasaṅkhāte
  lomahaṃsasutte 4- viriyaṃ 5-.
      [503] Tattha viriyaṃ brahmacariyanti adhippetaṃ. Viriyabrahmacariyassa
  hi idameva suttaṃ 6-. Tametaṃ etasmiṃ attabhāve catubbidhassa dukkarassa
  katattā caturaṅgasamannāgatanti vuttaṃ. Tapassī sudaṃ homīti sudanti

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. khu. jā. dasaka. 27/289. 2. jātakaṭṭhakathā. 5/492. 3. caritāti pāli.
  4. Ma. mū. 12/155. 5. Sī. mahāsīhanādasutte viriyaṃ.
  6. Sī. viriyabrahmacariyassāti idameva yuttaṃ.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 394]
  Nipātamattaṃ. Tapanissitako homīti atthoti tabbaṇṇanā 1-.
      Idameva suttaṃ āgataṭṭhānanti adhippāyo. Tepiṭake buddhavacane
  idameva suttapadaṃ viriyabrahmacariyassa āgataṭṭhānanti attho.
  Viriyañhi tasmiṃ visaye uttamaṃ paramukkaṃsagataṃ tādisacariyahetu cāti
  brahmacariyanti idha vuttaṃ. Caturaṅgasamannāgatanti catubbidhadukkarakiriyāya
  sādhakassa catubbidhassa attano pavattatiākārassa vasena
  caturaṅgasamannāgatanti taṭṭīkā.
      Catubbidhaṃ dukkarantu mahāsīhanādasutte oloketabbaṃ.
    [504] Hīnena brahmacariyena khattiye upapajjati
          majjhimena ca devattaṃ uttamena visujjhatīti
  nimijātake 2- attadamanavasena kato aṭṭhaṅgiko uposatho.
      [505] Tattha puthutitthāyatanesu methunaviratimattaṃ sīlaṃ hīnaṃ nāma
  tena khattiye upapajjati. Jhānassa upacāramattaṃ majjhimaṃ nāma
  tena devattaṃ upapajjati. Aṭṭhasamāpattinibbattakampana uttamaṃ nāma
  tena brahmaloke nibbattati. Taṃ bāhirakā nibbānanti kathenti.
  Tenāha 3- visujjhatīti. Imasmiṃ pana sāsane parisuddhasīlassa bhikkhuno
  aññataraṃ devanikāyaṃ paṭṭhentassa brahmacariyaṃ cetanāhīnatāya hīnaṃ
  nāma tena yathāpaṭṭhite devaloke nibbattati. Parisuddhasīlassa
  pana aṭṭhasamāpattinibbattanaṃ majjhimaṃ nāma tena brahmaloke
  nibbattati. Parisuddhasīlassa vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhetvā arahattappatti

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. pa. sū. 2/57. 2. khu. jā. mahā. 28/199. 3. tena.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 395]
  Uttamaṃ nāma tena visujjhatīti tabbaṇṇanā 1-.
      Attadamanavasenāti yathāpaṭiññaṃ arahantānaṃ anukaraṇākārena
  pavattadamanavasena manacchaṭṭhānaṃ indriyānaṃ damanenāti atthoti
  mahāsīhanādaṭīkā.
      [506] Katamañca bhikkhave brahmacariyaṃ ayameva ariyo aṭṭhaṅgiko
  maggo seyyathīdaṃ sammādiṭṭhīti saṃyuttamahāvagge brahmacariyasutte 2-
  ariyamaggo.
      Mahāsīhanādaṭīkāyaṃ sikkhāpattaseṭṭhacariyatāya ariyamaggo
  brahmacariyanti vuttaṃ.
      [507] Na tāvāhaṃ pāpima parinibbāyissāmi yāva na me idaṃ
  brahmacariyaṃ iddhañceva bhavissati phītañca vitthārikaṃ bahujaññaṃ puthubhūtaṃ
  yāva devamanussehi suppakāsitanti mahāparinibbānasutte 3- sāsanaṃ
  brahmacariyaṃ nāma.
      [508] Brahmacariyanti sikkhāttayasaṅgahitaṃ sakalasāsanaṃ brahmacariyaṃ.
  Iddhanti samiddhaṃ jhānassādavasena 4-. Phītanti vuḍḍhippattaṃ
  sabbaphāliphullaṃ viya abhiññāsampattivasena. Vitthārikanti vitthataṃ
  tasmiṃ tasmiṃ disābhāge patiṭṭhitavasena. Bahujaññanti bahūhi
  ñātaṃ paṭividdhaṃ mahājanābhisamayavasena. Puthubhūtanti sabbākāravasena
  puthulabhāvappattaṃ. Kathaṃ. Yāva devamanussehi suppakāsitanti

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. jātakaṭṭhakathā. 9/166. 2. saṃ. mahā. 19/31.
  3. dī. mahā.10/124. 4. jhānādivasenāti aṭṭhakathāyaṃ dissati.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 396]
  Yattakā viññujātikā devā ceva manussā ca atthi sabbehi suṭṭhu
  pakāsitanti atthoti tabbaṇṇanā 1- aṭṭhakaṅguttare dutiyapaṇṇāsakavaṇṇanā 2-
  ca.
      [509] Sikkhāttayasaṅgahitanti adhisīlasikkhādisikkhāttayasaṅgahaṃ.
  Sakalasāsanaṃ brahmacariyanti anavasesaṃ satthusāsanabhūtaṃ seṭṭhacariyaṃ.
  Samiddhanti sammadeva vaḍḍhitaṃ. Jhānassādavasenāti tehi tehi bhikkhūhi
  samadhigatajjhānasukhavasena. Vuḍḍhippattanti uḷārappaṇītabhāvūpagamanena
  sabbaso parivuḍḍhimupagataṃ sabbaphāliphullaṃ viya abhiññāsampattivasena
  abhiññāsampadāhi sāsanābhivuḍḍhiyā matthakappattito. Patiṭṭhitavasenāti
  patiṭṭhānavasena patiṭṭhappattiyāti attho. Paṭivedhavasena
  bahuno janassa hitanti bahujaññaṃ. Tenāha bahūhi ... Janābhisamayavasenāti.
  Puthu puthulaṃ bhūtaṃ jātaṃ puthu vā puthuttaṃ bhūtaṃ pavattanti
  puthubhūtaṃ. Tenāha sabbā ... Bhāvappattanti. Suṭṭhu pakāsitanti
  sammadeva ādikalyāṇādibhāvena paveditanti taṭṭīkā.
      Evaṃ sāmaññaphalaitivuttakavaṇṇanānayena dasavidhaṃ hoti brahmacariyaṃ.
      [510] Mahāsīhanādavaṇṇanāyantu dhammadesanajjhāsaye
  pakkhipitvā dvādasavidhaṃ vuttaṃ.
      Ekasmiṃ brahmacariyasmiṃ sahassaṃ maccuhāyinoti
  ettha hi dhammadesanā brahmacariyaṃ nāma.

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. su. vi. 2/203. 2. mano. pū. 3/313.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 397]
      Tattha brahmacariyasminti dhammadesanāyaṃ. Sā hi veneyyānaṃ
  brahmabhāvāvahanato brahmaṃ seṭṭhaṃ cariyaṃ brahmuno vā bhagavato
  vācasikaṃ cariyanti brahmacariyanti vuccati. Sahassaṃ maccuhāyinoti
  sahassamattā arahattasamadhigamena maccuvisayātikkamena maccuppahāyino
  jātāti mahāsīhanādaṭīkā.
    [511] Api ataramānānaṃ phalāsā va samijjhati
          vipakkabrahmacariyosmi evaṃ jānāhi gāmaṇīti
      ettha ajjhāsayo.
      Tattha ataramānānanti na taramānānaṃ desanākālaṃ udakkhantānaṃ.
  Phalāsā va samijjhatīti sudullabhaphalāpi āsā sammāpayogamanvāya
  ijjhatieva. Vipakkabrahmacariyosmīti visesena nipphannappaṇītajjhāsayo
  paripuṇṇauḷāramanoratho. So hi seṭṭhamano
  samācāratāya brahmacariyapariyāyena vuttoti mahāsīhanādaṭīkā.
      [512] Tattha dhammadesanā atthato sikkhāttayasaṅgahitasāsanadhammova
  kimassā visuṅgahaṇenāti sāmaññaphalādivaṇṇanāyaṃ vuttā.
  Yañca ajjhāsayo brahmacariyanti vuttaṃ taṃ ekanipātassa paṭhamavagge
  gāmaṇijātakavaṇṇanāya na sameti.
      Tattha 1- hi apīti nipātamattaṃ. Ataramānānanti paṇḍitānaṃ
  ovāde ṭhatvā aturitvā avegāyitvā upāyena kammaṃ karontānaṃ.
  Phalāsā vāti āsāphalaṃ yathāpaṭṭhitaṃ phalaṃ samijjhatiyevāti attho.

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. jātakaṭṭhakathā. 1/208.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 398]
  Vipakkabrahmacariyosmīti ettha cattāri saṅgahavatthūni seṭṭhacariyattā
  brahmacariyaṃ nāma. Tañca tammūlikāya yasasampattiyā paṭiladdhattā
  vipakkaṃ nāma. Yo ca yaso nipphanno sopi seṭṭhatthena brahmacariyaṃ
  nāmāti vuttaṃ.
      [513] Atīte hi bārāṇasīrañño puttasatassa sabbakaniṭṭho
  gāmaṇi nāma rājakumāro bodhisattaṃ sippasikkhāpakācariyaṃ nissāya
  pitu accayena rajjaṃ patvā tuṭṭho udānavasena taṃ gāthaṃ abhāsi.
  Tasmā gāmaṇīti attānaṃ sandhāyāha ambho gāmaṇi tvaṃ etaṃ
  kāraṇaṃ evaṃ jānāhi ācariyaṃ nissāya bhātikasataṃ atikkamitvā imaṃ
  mahārajjaṃ pattosmīti.
      [514] Idhaṭṭhakathāyantu bhagavati no āvuso brahmacariyaṃ
  vussatīti evamāvusoti rathavinītasutte 1- samaṇadhammo brahmacariyaṃ nāmāti
  attho vutto.
      Tattha bhagavati noti āvuso amhākaṃ bhagavato santike
  āyasmatā brahmacariyaṃ vussatīti rathavinītasuttavaṇṇanā 2-.
      Vussatīti vasiyatīti itivuttake dukanipātavaṇṇanā 3-.
      Vussatīti vusiyatīti atthoti catukkaṅguttare paṭhamapaṇṇāsakassa
  tatiyavagge brahmacariyasuttaṭīkā.
      Vasa nivāse vasati kamme uttaṃ pubbarūpattañca. Vussatīti
  ākhyātarūpasiddhi. Tattha pucchā dhammasenāpatissa evamāvusoti

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. Ma. mū. 12/290. 2. pa. sū. 2/209. 3. pa. dī. iti. 144.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 399]
  Paṭiññā puṇṇattherassāti.
      [515] Iti imañceva sāmaññaphalādivaṇṇanāyaṃ vuttañca gahetvā
  ekādasavidhaṃ brahmacariyaṃ bhavati. Tattha pana dānaṃ heṭṭhā sarūpato
  āgataṃ. Veyyāvaccuposathaṅgāni anavajjakammehi saṅgahitāni.
  Pañcasīlasadārasantosā pāpaviratimajjasaññamehi. Viriyaṃ tapena
  saṅgahitaṃ hoti. Appamaññāmethunaviratiariyamaggasāsanasamaṇadhammesu
  pana appamaññā khantiyā saṅgahetabbā. Adoso hi khanti nāma
  adosena ca mettā gahitā. Tāya gahitāya itarāpi tisso
  gahitvāva hontīti. Ariyamaggo parato vakkhamānena ariyasaccadassanena
  gayhamānattā idha na labbhati. Yathā hi surāpānaṃ parato
  majjapānena gayhamānattā āratī viratī pāpāti ettha na gahitaṃ
  evaṃ sampadamidaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ. Tenaṭṭhakathāyaṃ brahmacariyaṃ nāma
  methunaviratisamaṇadhammasāsanamaggānaṃ adhivacanaṃ. Idha pana ariyasaccadassanena
  parato maggassa gahitattā avasesaṃ sabbampi vaṭṭatīti
  vuttaṃ. Tattha yadi sāsanamidha gayhati puna ruttadoso āpajjati.
  Pariyattisāsanañhi subhāsitavācāya gahitameva. Pubbepi hi paresaṃ
  dhammadesanā vācā idha subhāsitā vācāti veditabbāti vuttaṃ. Tasmā
  yathā nirodhasaccaṃ ariyasaccānadassananti ettha gahitattā pacchā
  nibbānasacchikiriyāti ettha na gayhati evaṃ sampadamidaṃ veditabbaṃ.
      [516] Iti pārisesanayena methunaviratisamaṇadhammabrahmacariyameva
  labbhati tañcetaṃ uparupari nānappakāravisesādhigamahetuto maṅgalaṃ.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 400]
  Tatrīmāni vatthūni
      rājagahe kira kapilabrāhmaṇassa putto pipphali nāma māṇavo
  ahosi vīsativassiko mahāpuñño. Atha naṃ mātāpitaro gharabandhena
  bandhitukāmā lakkhaṇasampannaṃ itthiṃ pariyesāpetuṃ aṭṭha brāhmaṇe
  pesesuṃ. Te paṇṇākāraṃ gahetvā sāgalanagaraṃ agamaṃsu. Tasmiñca
  nagare bhaddakāpilānī nāma kosiyagottabrāhmaṇassa dhītā ahosi
  soḷasavassikā uttamarūpadharā mahāpuññā. Te tattha gantvā
  tassā mātāpitūnaṃ sabbaṃ pavuttiṃ kathetvā paṇṇākāraṃ datvā
  laddhā dārikāti kapilabrāhmaṇassa sāsanaṃ pesesuṃ. Māṇavopi
  sāsanaṃ sutvā anatthikova rahogato paṇṇaṃ likhi bhaddā attano
  jātigottabhogānurūpaṃ gharāvāsaṃ labhatu ahaṃ pabbajissāmi mā
  pacchā vippaṭisāriṇī ahosīti. Bhaddāpi teneva nayena paṇṇaṃ
  likhi. Dve paṇṇāni gahetvā āgacchantā antarāmagge
  samāgantvā dvepi vācetvā phāletvā araññeva chaḍḍetvā
  samānapaṇṇaṃ likhitvā agamaṃsu. Iti tesaṃ anicchantānaṃyeva samāgamo
  ahosi. Taṃdivasaṃyeva te dvepi bhuttasāyamāsā sayanaṃ āruhantāpi
  na ekato āruhanti. Māṇavo dakkhiṇapassena sayanaṃ āruhi.
  Bhaddāpi vāmapassena. Te aññamaññaṃ sarīrasamphassabhayena sayanamajjhe
  dve pupphadāmāni ṭhapetvā tiyāmarattiṃ asupantāva vītināmenti.
  Divā pana hasitamattaṃ na hoti. Te lokāmisena asaṃsaṭṭhā agāraṃ
  ajjhāvasantā mātāpitūnamaccayena samānacchandā hutvā ādittapaṇṇakuṭī
-------------------------------------------------
[page 401]
  Viya tayo bhavā pabbajissāmāti vatvā antarāpaṇato
  kāsāyāni mattikāpatte ca āharāpetvā aññamaññaṃ kese
  ohāretvā ye loke arahanto te uddissa amhākaṃ pabbajjāti
  pabbajitvā thavikāsu patte osāmetvā aṃse laggetvā pāsādato
  otaritvā pakkamiṃsu. Pipphali purato gacchanto dvedhāpathaṃ
  disvā tassa matthake ṭhito bhadde ime pabbajitvāpi vinā
  bhavituṃ na sakkontīti cintetvā amhesu duṭṭhacitto mahājano
  apāyapūrako bhaveyya imasmiṃ dvedhāpathe ekaṃ gaṇha ahaṃ ekena
  gamissāmīti āha. Āma ayya pabbajitānaṃ mātugāmo nāma malaṃ
  pabbajitvāpi vinā na bhavantīti amhākaṃ dosaṃ dassanti vinā
  bhavissāmāti tikkhattuṃ padakkhiṇaṃ katvā catūsu ṭhānesu pañcappatiṭṭhitena
  vanditvā añjaliṃ paggayha satasahassakappappamāṇe addhāne
  kato mittasanthavo ajja bhijjati tumhe dakkhiṇā nāma tumhākaṃ
  dakkhiṇamaggo vaṭṭati mayaṃ mātugāmā nāma vāmajātikā amhākaṃ
  vāmamaggoti vanditvā maggaṃ paṭipajji. Tesaṃ dvedhābhūtakāle ayaṃ
  mahāpaṭhavī tumhākaṃ guṇe dhāretuṃ na sakkomīti vadantī viya
  viravamānā akampi. Ākāse asanīsaddo viya vattati.
  Cakkavāḷapabbato unnadati. Satthā veḷuvane gandhakuṭiyaṃ nisinno
  paṭhavīkampanasaddaṃ sutvā āvajjento taṃ kāraṇaṃ ñatvā ekakova
  pattacīvaramādāya tigāvutamattaṃ paccuggamanaṃ katvā rājagahassa ca
  nāḷandāya ca antare bahuputtakanigrodhamūle nisīdi. Pipphalipi ekena
-------------------------------------------------
[page 402]
  Maggena gacchanto tattha nisinnaṃ satthāraṃ disvā sakkaccaṃ vanditvā
  nisinno nidānavagge kassapasaṃyutte vuttanayena tīhi ovādehi
  satthārā ovadito upasampanno hutvā sattāhaṃ puthujjano hutvā
  aṭṭhame divase arahattaṃ pāpuṇi. Bhaddāpi bhikkhunūpassayaṃ gantvā
  pacchā arahattaṃ pāpuṇi.
      Iti ete methunaviratibrahmacariyaṃ caritvā arahattaṃ pattā.
  Tasmā sā tesaṃ methunavirati maṅgalaṃ nāma. Ayamettha saṅkhepo.
  Vitthāro tu kassapasaṃyuttavaṇṇanādīsu oloketabbo.
                        Dvijanavatthu.
      [517] Saṭṭhimattā kira bhikkhū satthu santike kammaṭṭhānaṃ gahetvā
  vasanaṭṭhānaṃ pariyesantā kosalaraṭṭhe pabbatapāde mātikaṃ nāma gāmaṃ
  piṇḍāya pāvisiṃsu. Tassa pana gāmassa sāmiko mātiko nāma.
  Tassa mātā mātikamātāti vissutā. Sā te bhikkhū disvā
  pasannacittā bhikkhaṃ datvā kattha gantukāmatthāti pucchi.
  Yathāphāsukaṭṭhānaṃ upāsiketi. Sā sace ayyā idha temāsaṃ vasissanti
  ahaṃ saraṇattayaṃ pañcasīlañca gahetvā uposathakammaṃ karissāmīti
  vatvā tesu adhivāsentesu vasanaṭṭhānaṃ paṭijaggitvā adāsi. Te
  tattha vasantā ekato anisīditvā sakasakaṭṭhānesu dvattiṃsākāre
  sajjhāyaṃ katvā attabhāve khayavayaṃ paṭṭhapetvā nisīdiṃsu. Athekadivasaṃ
  mātikamātā sappiphāṇitādīni gāhāpetvā sāyaṇhasamaye vihāraṃ
  gantvā bhikkhū adisvā kahaṃ nu kho ayyāti purise pucchi.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 403]
  Sakasakaṭṭhānesu nisinnā bhavissanti ayyeti. Kinnu kho katvā daṭṭhuṃ
  sakkhissāmīti. Atha naṃ bhikkhūnaṃ katikavattaññū manussā gaṇḍiyā
  pahaṭāya ayyā sannipatissanti ayyeti vadiṃsu. Sā gaṇḍiṃ paharāpesi.
  Taṃ sutvā bhikkhū kassaci aphāsukaṃ bhavissatīti sakasakaṭṭhānato nikkhamma
  vihāramajjhe sannipatiṃsu. Dvepi janā ekamaggenāgatā nāma natthi.
  Sā te disvā imehi aññamaññaṃ kalaho kato bhavissatīti cintetvā
  pucchi. Na mayaṃ kalahaṃ karimhā apica kho mayaṃ sakasakaṭṭhāne nisīditvā
  samaṇadhammaṃ karimhāti. Ko esa bhante samaṇadhammo nāmāti.
  Dvattiṃsākāre sajjhāyo attabhāve ca khayavayaṃ paṭṭhapanāti.
  Kimesa bhante tumhākameva vaṭṭati udāhu amhākampīti. Kassacipi
  avārito esa upāsiketi. Tenahi bhante taṃ ācikkhathāti. Te taṃ
  uggaṇhāpesuṃ. Sā tato paṭṭhāya samaṇadhammaṃ karontī tehi
  puretarameva heṭṭhimaphalattayaṃ pāpuṇi. Maggenevassā paṭisambhidā
  lokiyābhiññā ca āgatā. Sā maggaphalasukhato vuṭṭhāya dibbacakkhunā
  upadhārentī tesaṃ puthujjanattanaṃ disvā kintesaṃ asappāyanti
  upadhārentī āhārasappāyābhāvaṃ disvā sappāyāhāraṃ sajjetvā
  tesaṃ adāsi. Tesaṃ sappāyāhāralābhena cittaṃ ekaggaṃ jātaṃ.
  Te ekaggacittā vipassanaṃ vaḍḍhetvā saha paṭisambhidāhi arahattaṃ
  pāpuṇiṃsu. Iti tepi samaṇadhammabrahmacariyaṃ caritvā maggaphalaṃ
  pattā. Tasmā so tesaṃ samaṇadhammo maṅgalaṃ nāma.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 404]
      Dhammapadassa cittavagge dunniggahassa lahunoti gāthāvaṇṇanāyaṃ
  saṭṭhibhikkhuvatthu.
                      Brahmacariyakathā.
                      -----------
-------------------------------------------------
[page 405]
                      Ariyasaccadassanakathā
      [518] Ariyasaccāni nāma dukkhādīni cattāri tathattā saccāni.
  Vuttañhi mahāvāravagge 1- saccasaṃyuttassa dutiyavagge cattārīmāni
  bhikkhave tathāni avitathāni anaññathāni. Katamāni cattāri. Idaṃ
  dukkhanti bhikkhave tathametaṃ avitathametaṃ anaññathametaṃ ayaṃ
  dukkhasamudayoti ayaṃ dukkhanirodhoti ayaṃ dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadāti
  tathametaṃ ... Anaññathametanti.
                       Tathasuttapāli.
      [519] Sabhāvāvijahanatthena tathaṃ. Dukkhañhi dukkhameva.
  Vuttasabhāvassa amoghatāya avitathaṃ. Na hi dukkhaṃ adukkhaṃ nāma hoti.
  Aññasabhāvānupagamanena anaññathaṃ. Na hi dukkhaṃ samudayādīnaṃ sabhāvaṃ
  upagacchati. Samudayādīsupi eseva nayoti tabbaṇṇanā 2-.
      Sabhāvāvijahanatthenāti attano dukkhasabhāvassa kadāci
  apariccajanena tathaṃ taṃsabhāvaṃ. Tenāha dukkhañhi dukkhamevāti.
  Vuttasabhāvassāti vuttadukkhasabhāvassa. Amoghatāyāti avañcatāya.
  Avitathanti tathaṃ. Tenāha na hi dukkhaṃ adukkhaṃ nāma hotīti.
  Aññasabhāvānupagamanenāti samudayādisabhāvānupagamanena.
  Anaññathanti anaññasabhāvaṃ. Tenāha na hītiādinti taṭṭīkā.
      [520] Ariyānaṃ saccānīti ariyasaccāni. Vuttañhi tasseva

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. Sī. mahāvāra. 19/539. 2. sā. pa. 3/410.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 406]
  Tatiyavagge 1- imāni kho bhikkhave cattāri ariyasaccāni tathāni avitathāni
  anaññathāni tasmā ariyānaṃ saccānīti 2- vuccantīti.
                       Tathasuttapāli.
      [521] Tasmā ariyānaṃ saccānīti yasmā tathāni avitathāni
  anaññathāni tasmā ariyānaṃ saccānīti vuccanti. Na hi vitathāni
  ariyasaccato paṭivijjhantīti tabbaṇṇanā 3-.
      Ariyānanti buddhādīnaṃ ariyānaṃ. Tenāha na hītiādinti
  taṭṭīkā.
      [522] Apica ariyassa bhagavato saccānītipi ariyasaccāni.
  Vuttañhi tattheva 4- cattārīmāni bhikkhave ariyasaccāni ...
  Dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasaccaṃ sadevake bhikkhave loke samārake
  ... Sadevamanussāya tathāgato ariyo tasmā ariyasaccānīti
  vuccantīti.
                       Lokasuttapāli.
      [523] Tathāgato ariyo tasmā ariyasaccānīti yasmā
  ariyena tathāgatena paṭividdhattā desitattā ca tāni ariyasantakāni
  honti tasmā ariyassa saccattā ariyasaccānīti atthoti
  tabbaṇṇanā 5-.
      Paṭividdhattā desitattā cāti iminā paṭivedhaññāṇena

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. saṃ. mahāvāra. 19/545. 2. ariyasaccānītipi. pāli.
  3-4. sā. pa. 3/411. 5. saṃ. mahāvāra. 19/535.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 407]
  Desanāñāṇena ca pariggahitattā ariyassa santakāni hontīti ariyassa
  bhagavato santakabhāvaṃ dassetīti taṭṭīkā.
      [524] Athavā etesaṃ abhisambuddhattā ariyabhāvasiddhitopi
  ariyasaccāni. Vuttañhi tattheva 1- imesaṃ kho bhikkhave catunnaṃ
  ariyasaccānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ abhisambuddhattā tathāgato arahaṃ sammāsambuddhoti
  vuccatīti.
      Saccavibhaṅgavaṇṇanāyampi 2- imamevatthaṃ vatvā puna apica ariyāni
  saccānīti ariyasaccāni. Ariyānīti avitathāni avisaṃvādakānīti
  atthoti vuttaṃ.
      Ariyabhāvasiddhitopīti ettha ariyasādhakāni saccāni ariyasaccānīti
  purimapade uttarapadalopo daṭṭhabbo. Ariyāni saccānīti ettha
  avitathabhāvena araṇīyattā adhigantabbattā ariyāni. Ariyavohāro
  vā ayaṃ avisaṃvādako avitatharūpo daṭṭhabboti vibhaṅgaṭīkā.
      [525] Yasmā vā sadevakena lokena araṇīyato abhigamanīyatoti
  vuttaṃ hoti vāyamitabbayuttaṭṭhānasaññite aye vā īriyanato
  anaye vā anīriyanato sattattiṃsabodhipakkhiyāriyadhammasammāyogato vā
  ariyasammatā buddhappaccekabuddhasāvakā etāni paṭivijjhanti tasmā
  ariyasaccānīti vuccantīti khuddakapāṭhe kumārapañhavaṇṇanā 3-.
      Yasmā panetāni buddhādayo ariyā paṭivijjhanti tasmā

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. saṃ. mahāvāra. 19/543. 2. sam. vi. 109. 3. pa. jo. khu. khu. 89.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 408]
  Ariyasaccānīti saccavibhaṅgavaṇṇanā 1-.
      Buddhādayo ariyā paṭivijjhantīti ettha paṭividdhakāle pavattaṃ
  buddhātivohāraṃ agamā rājagahaṃ buddhotiādīsu viya purimakāleva
  āropetvā buddhādayoti vuttaṃ. Te hi buddhādayo catūhi
  maggehi paṭivijjhantīti ariyappaṭivijjhitabbāni saccāni ariyasaccānīti
  cettha purimapade uttarapadalopo daṭṭhabboti vibhaṅgaṭīkā.
      [526] Yantu kumārapañhavibhaṅgavaṇṇanāsu 2- ariyā imāni
  paṭivijjhanti tasmā ariyasaccānīti vuccantīti suttapadaṃ niddiṭṭhaṃ
  taṃ saccasaṃyutte na niddiṭṭhaṃ. Itivuttake ekanipātassa tatiyavagge
  aṭṭhisañcayasuttavaṇṇanāyampi 3- ariyasaddavacanattho vutto.
      Tattha purimāni dve saccāni vaṭṭaṃ pacchimāni vivaṭṭaṃ. Tesu
  bhikkhuno vaṭṭe kammaṭṭhānābhiniveso hoti vivaṭṭe natthi abhiniveso.
  Purimāni hi dve saccāni pañcakkhandhā dukkhaṃ taṇhā samudayoti
  evaṃ saṅkhepena ca katame pañcakkhandhā rūpakkhandhotiādinā nayena
  vitthārena ca ācariyassa santike uggaṇhitvā vācāya punappunaṃ
  parivattento yogāvacaro kammaṃ karoti. Itaresu pana dvīsu
  saccesu nirodhasaccaṃ iṭṭhaṃ kantaṃ manāpaṃ maggasaccaṃ iṭṭhaṃ kantaṃ
  manāpanti evaṃ savaneneva kammaṃ karoti. So evaṃ karonto
  cattāri saccāni ekena paṭivedhena paṭivijjhati ekābhisamayena abhisameti
  dukkhaṃ pariññāpaṭivedhena paṭivijjhati samudayaṃ pahānappaṭivedhena

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. sam. vi. 109. 2. pa. jo. khu. khu. 90. 3. pa. dī. iti. 111.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 409]
  Nirodhasaccaṃ sacchikiriyāpaṭivedhena maggaṃ bhāvanāpaṭivedhena paṭivijjhati
  dukkhaṃ pariññābhisamayena ... Maggaṃ bhāvanābhisamayena abhisameti.
  Evamassa pubbabhāge dvīsu 1- uggahaparipucchāsavanadhāraṇasammasanappaṭivedho
  hoti dvīsu savanappaṭivedhoyeva aparabhāge tīsu kiccato paṭivedho
  hoti nirodhe ārammaṇappaṭivedhoti mahāvagge maggasaṃyuttassa
  paṭhamavagge aṭṭhamasuttavaṇṇanā 2- saccavibhaṅgavaṇṇanā 3- ca.
      [527] Kammaṭṭhānābhinivesoti kammaṭṭhānappaṭipatti. Purimāni
  dve saccāni uggaṇhitvāti sambandho. Iṭṭhaṃ kantaṃ manāpanti
  nirodhamaggesu ninnabhāvaṃ dasseti na aninnataṃ. Tanninnabhāvoeva
  ca tattha kammakaraṇaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ. Ekeneva ñāṇena saccānaṃ
  paṭivedhanimittattā vā so eva abhimukhabhāvena tehi samiti samāgamoti
  ekābhisamayo. Assāti ñāṇassa yogino vā. Ettha ca
  ye keci lokikā na 4- paṭivedhova visayayāthāvabodhābhāvato nanu
  uggahādippaṭivedhopi paṭivedho na so lokuttaroti. Tanna
  kevalena paṭivedhasaddena uggahādippaṭivedhānaṃ avacanīyattā.
  Paṭivedhanimittattā vā uggahādivasena pavattaṃ dukkhādīsu pubbabhāge ñāṇaṃ
  paṭivedhoti vuttaṃ na paṭivedhabhūtattā paṭivedhabhūtameva 5- pana ñāṇaṃ
  ujukaṃ paṭivedhoti vattabbataṃ arahati. Kiccatoti pariññādikiccato.
  Ārammaṇappaṭivedhoti sacchikiriyāpaṭivedhamāhāti maggasaṃyuttaṭīkā.

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. ito paraṃ saccesūti pāṭho aṭṭhakathāyaṃ likhiyati. 2. sā. pa. 3/203.
  3. sam. vi. 150. 4. Sī. nasaddo natthi. 5. Sī. na paṭivedhabhūtameva.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 410]
      [528] Sabbampi paṭivedhaññāṇaṃ lokuttaraṃ savanadhāraṇasammasanaññāṇaṃ
  lokiyaṃ kāmāvacaraṃ. Paccavekkhaṇā pana maggasaccassa
  hotīti saccavibhaṅgavaṇṇanā 1-.
      Idhāpi paṭivedhavasena saccadassanaṃ labbhati. Tenaṭṭhakathāyaṃ 2-
  ariyasaccānadassanaṃ nāma catunnaṃ ariyasaccānaṃ abhisamayavasena
  maggadassanaṃ taṃ saṃsāradukkhavītikkamahetuto maṅgalanti vuccatīti vuttaṃ.
      [529] Abhisamayavasenāti cetaṃ ekābhisamayaṃ sandhāya vuttaṃ.
  Tenāha yo bhikkhave dukkhaṃ passati dukkhasamudayampi so passati
  dukkhanirodhampi passati dukkhanirodhagāminīpaṭipadampi passati yo
  dukkhasamudayaṃ passati dukkhampi so passati ... Yo dukkhanirodhaṃ passati
  dukkhampi so passati ... Yo dukkhanirodhagāminīpaṭipadaṃ passati dukkhampi
  so passati dukkhasamudayampi passati dukkhanirodhampi passatīti.
         Saccasaṃyuttassa tatiyavagge gavampatisuttapāli 3-.
      [530] Yo bhikkhave dukkhaṃ passati dukkhasamudayampi so
  passatītiādi ekappaṭivedhavasena vuttaṃ. Imasmiṃ hi sutte ekappaṭivedho
  kathitoti tabbaṇṇanā 4-.
      Ekappaṭivedhoti ekeneva ñāṇena catunnaṃ saccānaṃ ekajjhaṃ
  paṭivedhoti taṭṭīkā.
      [531] Yo hi catunnaṃ saccānaṃ na passati so dukkhaṃ

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. sam. vi. 151. 2. pa. jo. khu. khu. 167. 3. saṃ. mahāvāra. 19/547.
  4. sā. pa. 3/411.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 411]
  Nātikkamati. Tādisassa hi dīgho saṃsāro. Tenāha saccasaṃyuttassa
  tatiyavagge 1- catunnaṃ bhikkhave ariyasaccānaṃ ananubodhā appaṭivedhā
  evamidaṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ sandhāvitaṃ saṃsaritaṃ mamañceva tumhākañca.
  Katamesaṃ catunnaṃ. Dukkhassa bhikkhave ariyasaccassa ananubodhā
  appaṭivedhā evamidaṃ ... Tumhākañca dukkhasamudayassa bhikkhave ariyasaccassa
  ... Dukkhanirodhassa bhikkhave ariyasaccassa ... Dukkhanirodhagāminīpaṭipadāya
  bhikkhave ariyasaccassa ananubodhā appaṭivedhā evamidaṃ ... Tumhākañca.
  Tayidaṃ bhikkhave dukkhaṃ ariyasaccaṃ anubuddhaṃ paṭividdhaṃ dukkhasamudayo
  dukkhanirodho dukkhanirodhagāminī paṭipadā ariyasaccaṃ anubuddhaṃ
  paṭividdhaṃ. Ucchinnā bhavataṇhā khīṇā bhavanetti natthīdāni
  punabbhavoti. Idamavoca bhagavā idaṃ vatvāna sugato athāparaṃ
  etadavoca satthā
      catunnaṃ ariyasaccānaṃ yathābhūtaṃ adassanā
      saṃsaritaṃ dīghamaddhānaṃ tāsu tāsveva jātisu
      tāni 2- etāni diṭṭhāni bhavanetti samūhatā
      ucchinnaṃ mūlaṃ dukkhassa natthīdāni punabbhavoti.
                  Paṭhamageyyasuttaṃ 3-.
      [532] Ananubodhāti ananubujjhanena. Appaṭivedhāti
  appaṭivijjhanenāti tabbaṇṇanā 4-.

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. saṃ. mahāvāra. 19/541. 2. yānīti pāliyaṃ dissati.
  3. saṃ. mahāvāra. 19/539. 4. sā. pa. 3/411.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 412]
      Ananubodhāti paṭivedhassa anurūpaṃ bodhābhāvena. Appaṭivedhāti
  saccānaṃ paṭimukhaṃ ativijjhavedhābhāvenāti taṭṭīkā.
      [533] Ananubodhāti abujjhanena ajānanena. Appaṭivedhāti
  appaṭivijjhanena appaccakkhakiriyāya. Dīghamaddhānanti ciraṃ kālaṃ.
  Sandhāvitanti bhavato bhavagamanena sandhāvitaṃ. Saṃsaritanti punappunaṃ
  gamanāgamanavasena saṃsaritaṃ. Mamañceva tumhākañcāti mayā ca
  tumhehi ca. Athavā sandhāvitaṃ saṃsaritanti sandhāvanaṃ saṃsaraṇaṃ
  mama ceva tumhākañca ahosi. Bhavataṇhāti bhavesu taṇhā.
  Bhavanettīti bhavarajju. Taṇhāyaeva taṃ nāmaṃ. Tāya hi sattā
  goṇā viya gīvāyaṃ bandhitvā taṃ taṃ bhavaṃ niyyanti tasmā bhavanettīti
  vuccatīti catukkaṅguttare sabbappaṭhamasuttavaṇṇanā 1-.
      [534] Anubodhoti pubbabhāgiyaṃ ñāṇaṃ. Paṭivedhoti
  maggaññāṇena abhisamayo. Tattha yasmā anubodhapubbako paṭivedho
  anubodhena vinā na hoti anubodhopi ekacco paṭivedhasambandho
  tadubhayābhāvahetukañca vaṭṭe saṃsaraṇaṃ tasmā vuttaṃ pāliyaṃ ananubodhā
  ... Tumhākañcāti. Paṭisandhiggahaṇavasena bhavato bhavantarūpagamanaṃ
  sandhāvanaṃ aparāparaṃ cavanūpapajjanavasena sañcaraṇaṃ saṃsaraṇanti āha
  bhavatotiādiṃ. Sandhāvitasaṃsaritapadānaṃ kammasādhanataṃ sandhāyāha mayā
  ca tamhehi cāti paṭhamavikappe. Dutiyavikappe pana bhāvasādhanataṃ
  hadaye katvā mama ceva tumhākañcāti yathāvuttavaseneva vuttaṃ.

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. mano. pū. 2/325.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 413]
  Dīgharajjunā 1- bandhaṃ sakuṇaṃ viya rajjuhattho puriso desantaraṃ taṇhārajjunā 2-
  bandhaṃ sattasantānaṃ abhisaṅkhāro bhavantaraṃ neti etāyāti
  bhavanetti. Tenāha bhavarajjūtiādinti taṭṭīkā.
      [535] Tattheva dutiye geyyasuttepi 3- vuttaṃ bhagavatā bhikkhūnaṃ
  dhammaṃ desentena
      ye dukkhaṃ nappajānanti atho dukkhassa sambhavaṃ
      yattha ca sabbaso dukkhaṃ asesaṃ uparujjhati
      tañca maggaṃ na jānanti dukkhūpasamagāminaṃ
      cetovimuttihīnā te atho paññāvimuttiyā
      abhabbā te antakiriyāya te ve jātijarūpagā.
      Ye ca dukkhaṃ pajānanti atho dukkhassa sambhavaṃ
      yattha ca sabbaso dukkhaṃ asesaṃ uparujjhati
      tañca maggaṃ pajānanti dukkhūpasamagāminaṃ
      cetovimuttisampannā atho paññāvimuttiyā
      bhabbā te antakiriyāya na te jātijarūpagāti.
  Suttanipātassa tatiyavagge dvayatānupassanāsuttepi 4- tatheva vuttaṃ.
      [536] Tattha yattha cāti nibbānaṃ dasseti. Nibbāne hi
  dukkhaṃ sabbaso uparujjhati sabbappakāraṃ uparujjhati sahetukaṃ uparujjhati
  asesaṃ uparujjhati. Tañca magganti tañca aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ magganti

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1-2.... rajjuyāti bhavitabbaṃ. 3. saṃ. mahāvāra. 19/542. 4. khu. su. 25/474.

[page 414]
  Dvayatānupassanāsuttavaṇṇanā 1-.
      Dukkhassa upasamaṃ nirodhaṃ gacchatīti dukkhūpasamagāminanti itivuttake
  aṭṭhisañcayasuttavaṇṇanā 2-.
      [537] Cetovimutti paññāvimuttīti phalasamādhiphalappaññānaṃ
  nāmanti saccasaṃyuttavaṇṇanā 3-.
      Phalasamādhiphalappaññānanti maggaphalasamādhippaññānanti taṭṭīkā.
      Arahattaphalasamādhi rāgavirāgā cetovimutti arahattaphalappaññā
  avijjāvirāgā paññāvimuttīti veditabbā. Taṇhācaritena
  vā appanājhānabalena kilese vikkhambhitvā adhigataṃ arahattaphalaṃ
  rāgavirāgā cetovimutti diṭṭhicaritena upacārajjhānamattaṃ nibbattetvā
  vipassitvā adhigataṃ arahattaphalaṃ avijjāvirāgā paññāvimutti.
  Anāgāmiphalaṃ vā kāmarāgavirāgā cetovimutti arahattaphalaṃ sabbākārato
  avijjāvirāgā paññāvimuttīti. Antakiriyāyāti vaṭṭadukkhassa
  antakaraṇāya. Jātijarūpagāti jātijaraṃ upagatā jātijarāya vā
  upagatā na parimuccanti jātijarāyāti dvayatānupassanāsuttavaṇṇanā 4-.
      Diṭṭhasaccā pana saṃsāradukkhaṃ tiṇṇā gaṇanapathaṃ vītivattā.
  Kiṃ vatthunā.
                     Ariyasaccadassanakathā.

[page 415]
                     Nibbānasacchikiriyākathā
      [538] Nibbānaṃ nāma pariyāyena duvidhaṃ hoti. Tenāha
  itivuttake dutiyavaggassa sattamasutte 1- dvemā bhikkhave
  nibbānadhātuyo. Katamā dve. Saupādisesā ca nibbānadhātu anupādisesā
  ca nibbānadhātu. Katamā ca bhikkhave saupādisesā nibbānadhātu.
  Idha bhikkhave bhikkhu arahaṃ hoti khīṇāsavo vusitavā katakaraṇīyo
  ohitabhāro anuppattasadattho parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyojano sammadaññā
  vimutto. Tassa tiṭṭhanteva pañcindriyāni yesaṃ avigatattā 2-
  manāpāmanāpaṃ paccanubhoti sukhadukkhaṃ paccanubhoti 3-. Tassa yo
  rāgakkhayo dosakkhayo mohakkhayo ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave saupādisesā
  nibbānadhātu. Katamā ca bhikkhave anupādisesā nibbānadhātu. Idha
  bhikkhave bhikkhu arahaṃ hoti ... Sammadaññā vimutto. Tassa
  idheva bhikkhave sabbavedayitāni anabhinanditāni sītibhavissanti ayaṃ
  vuccati bhikkhave anupādisesā nibbānadhātu. Imā kho bhikkhave dve
  nibbānadhātuyoti. Etamatthaṃ bhagavā avoca. Tatthetaṃ iti vuccati
                dve imā cakkhumatā pakāsitā
                nibbānadhātū anissitena tādinā
                ekā hi dhātu idha diṭṭhadhammikā
                saupādisesā bhavanettisaṅkhayā
                anupādisesā pana samparāyikā

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. khu. iti. 25/258. 2. pāliyaṃ avighātattāti pāṭho. 3. paṭisaṃvedayatīti.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 416]
                Yamhi nirujjhanti bhavāni sabbaso
                ye etadaññāya padaṃ asaṅkhataṃ
                vimuttacittā bhavanettisaṅkhayā
                te dhammasārādhigamā khaye 1- ratā
                pahaṃsu vo 2- sabbabhavāni tādinoti.
                          Pāli.
      [539] Dvemāti dve imā. Vānaṃ vuccati taṇhā nikkhantaṃ
  vānato natthi vā ettha vānaṃ imasmiṃ vā adhigate vānassa
  abhāvoti nibbānaṃ. Tadeva nissattanijjīvatthena sabhāvadhāraṇatthena
  ca dhātūti nibbānadhātu. Nibbānadhātuyā paramatthato bhedo natthi
  pariyāyena pana paññāyatīti taṃ pariyāyabhedaṃ sandhāya dvemā
  bhikkhave nibbānadhātuyoti vatvā yathādhippetaṃ pabhedaṃ dassetuṃ
  saupādisesātiādi vuttaṃ. Tattha taṇhādīhi phalabhāvena upādiyatīti
  upādi khandhapañcakaṃ. Upādiyeva seso upādiseso. Saha
  upādisesenāti saupādiseso. Tadabhāvato anupādisesoti
  itivuttakavaṇṇanā 3-.
      [540] Arahanti ārakakkileso dūrakkileso pahīnakkilesoti
  attho. Khīṇāsavoti cattāro āsavā arahato khīṇā pahīnā
  samucchinnā paṭippassaddhā abhabbuppattikā ñāṇagginā daḍḍhā
  tena vuccati khīṇāsavoti. Vusitavāti garusaṃvāsepi ariyamaggepi

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. pāliyaṃ dhammasārādhigamakkhayeti pāṭho. 2. teti. 3. pa. dī. iti. 215.

[page 417]
  Dasasu ariyavāsesupi vasi parivasi vuttho parivuttho vutthāvāso
  ciṇṇacaraṇoti vusitavā. Katakaraṇīyoti puthujjanakalyāṇakaṃ upādāya
  satta sekhā catūhi maggehi karaṇīyaṃ karonti nāma khīṇāsavassa
  sabbakaraṇīyāni katāni pariyositāni natthi uttariṃ karaṇīyaṃ
  dukkhakkhayādhigamāyāti katakaraṇīyo. Ohitabhāroti tayo bhārā
  khandhabhāro kisesabhāro abhisaṅkhārabhāro tassime tayo bhārā
  ohitā oropitā nikkhittā pātitā tena vuccati ohitabhāroti.
  Anuppattasadatthoti anuppatto sadatthaṃ sakatthanti vuttaṃ hoti.
  Kakārassāyaṃ dakāro kato. Sadatthoti ca arahattaṃ veditabbaṃ.
  Tañhi attūpanibandhatthena attano avijahanatthena attano paramatthatthena
  ca attano atthattā sakatthoti vuccati. Parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyojanoti
  dasa saṃyojanāni kāmarāgasaṃyojanaṃ paṭighamānadiṭṭhivicikicchāsīlabbata-
  parāmāsabhavarāgaissāmacchariyāvijjāsaṃyojanaṃ imāni hi satte bhavesu
  saṃyojenti upanibandhanti bhavaṃ vā bhavena saṃyojenti tasmā
  bhavasaṃyojanānīti vuccanti imāni bhavasaṃyojanāni arahato parikkhīṇāni
  pahīnāni ñāṇagginā daḍḍhāni tena vuccati parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyojanoti.
  Sammadaññāti sammā aññāya. Kiṃ vuttaṃ hoti. Khandhānaṃ
  khandhaṭṭhaṃ āyatanānaṃ āyatanaṭṭhaṃ dhātūnaṃ dhātuṭṭhaṃ dukkhassa
  pīḷanaṭṭhaṃ samudayassa pabhavaṭṭhaṃ nirodhassa santaṭṭhaṃ maggassa
  dassanaṭṭhaṃ sabbe saṅkhārā aniccāti evamādiṃ vā bhedaṃ sammā
  yathābhūtaṃ aññāya jānitvā tīrayitvā tulayitvā vibhāvetvā vibhūtaṃ
-------------------------------------------------
[page 418]
  Katvā. Vimuttoti dve vimuttiyo cittassa ca adhimutti
  nibbānañca. Arahā hi sabbakkilesehi vimuttacittattā
  cittavimuttiyāpi vimutto nibbānaṃ adhimuttattā nibbānepi
  vimutto tena ca sammadaññā vimuttoti mūlapariyāyavaṇṇanā 1-
  itivuttakavaṇṇanā 2- ca.
      [541] Kāmañcāyaṃ suttavaṇṇanā abhidhammanayo pana
  nippariyāyoti tena dassento cattāro āsavāti āha.
  Samucchinnā paṭippassaddhāti na kevalaṃ samucchinnā eva athakho
  paṭippassaddhāpīti maggakiccena saddhiṃ phalakiccampi niddhāreti.
  Sīlavisodhanā 3- garūnaṃ paṭipattiyā anukaraṇaṃ garusaṃvāso.
  Ariyamaggasampattipatti eva ariyamaggavāso. Dasāriyavāso nāma
  pañcaṅgavippahīnatādayo. Vussatīti vusitaṃ ariyamaggo ariyaphalañca taṃ
  etassa atthīti atisayavacanicchāvasena vusitavāti vutto. Karaṇīyanti
  pariññāpahānabhāvanāsacchikiriyamāha. Tampana yasmā catūhi maggehi
  paccekaṃ catūsu saccesu kattabbattā soḷasavidhaṃ veditabbaṃ tenāha
  catūhi maggehi karaṇīyanti. Bhārāti osīdāpanatthena bhārā.
  Vuttañhi bhārā have pañcakkhandhā teti 4-. Attano
  yonisomanasikārāyattanti attūpanibandhaṃ. Sasantānapariyāpannattā attano
  avijahanaṃ. Tayidaṃ yadipi sabbasmiṃ anavajjadhamme sambhavati

[page 419]
  Akuppasabhāvāparihānadhamme pana aggabhūte arahatte sātisayaṃ na itaresūti
  dassento āha attano paramatthatthenāti. Uttamatthabhāvenāti
  attho. Suttantanayo nāma pariyāyanayoti nippariyāyanayena saṃyojanāni
  dassento issāmacchariyasaṃyojananti āha na ca pana rūparāgotiādinā.
  Bhavesu saṃyojentīti kilesakammavipākavaṭṭānaṃ 1- paccayā
  hutvā nissarituṃ apadānavasena bandhanti satipi hi aññesaṃ
  tappaccayabhāve na vinā saṃyojanāni tesaṃ tappaccayabhāvo atthi
  orambhāgiyasaṅgahitoti taṃtaṃbhavanibbattakakammaniyamo bhavaniyamo hoti
  na ca ucchinnasaṃyojanassa katānipi kammāni bhavaṃ nibbattentīti
  tesaṃyeva saṃyojanattho daṭṭhabbo. Sammā aññāyāti ājānanabhūtāya
  aggamaggappaññāya sammā yathābhūtaṃ dukkhādīsu yo yathā jānitabbo
  taṃ tathā jānitvā. Cittavimutti sabbassa cittasaṅkilesassa vossaggo
  nibbānādhimutti nibbāne adhimuccanaṃ tattha ninnapoṇapabbhāratāti
  mūlapariyāyaṭīkā.
      [542] Tassa tiṭṭhantevāti tassa arahato purimabhave hetubhūtaṃ
  kammaṃ yāva na khīyati tāva tiṭṭhantiyeva cakkhvādīni pañcindriyāni.
  Avigatattāti anuppādanirodhavasena aniruddhattā. Manāpāmanāpanti
  iṭṭhāniṭṭhaṃ rūpādigocaraṃ. Paccanubhotīti vindati paṭilabhati. Sukhadukkhaṃ
  paṭisaṃvedetīti vipākabhūtaṃ sukhañca dukkhañca tehi dvārehi paṭilabhati.
  Ettāvatā upādisesaṃ dassetvā idāni saupādisesaṃ nibbānadhātuṃ

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. kilesakammavipākaṭṭhānaṃtipi.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 420]
  Dassetuṃ tassa yotiādi vuttaṃ. Tattha tassāti tassa
  saupādisesassa arahato. Rāgakkhayoti rāgassa khayo khīṇākāro
  abhāvo accantamanuppādo. Esa nayo sesesupi. Ettāvatā
  rāgādikkhayo saupādisesā nibbānadhātūti dassitaṃ hoti. Idhevāti
  imasmiṃyeva attabhāve. Sabbavedayitānīti sukhādayo sabbā
  abyākatavedanā. Kusalākusalavedanā pana pubbeyeva pahīnā.
  Anabhinanditānīti taṇhādīhi na abhinanditāni. Sītibhavissantīti
  accantavūpasamena saṅkhāradarathappaṭipassaddhiyā sītalībhavissanti
  appaṭisandhikanirodhena nirujjhissantīti attho. Na kevalaṃ vedayitāniyeva
  sabbepi pana khīṇāsavasantāne pañcakkhandhā nirujjhissanti.
  Vedayitasīsena desanā katā. Gāthāsu. Cakkhumatāti buddhacakkhu
  dhammacakkhu dibbacakkhu paññācakkhu samantacakkhūti pañcahi cakkhūhi
  cakkhumatā. Anissitenāti taṇhādiṭṭhinissayavasena kañci dhammaṃ
  anissitena rāgabandhanādīhi vā abandhena. Tādināti chaḷaṅgupekkhāvasena
  sabbattha iṭṭhādīsu ekasabhāvatāsaṅkhātena tādilakkhaṇena
  tādinā. Diṭṭhadhammikāti imasmiṃ attabhāve bhavā vattamānā.
  Bhavanettisaṅkhayāti bhavanettiyā taṇhāya parikkhayā. Samparāyikāti
  samparāye khandhabhedato parabhāge bhavā. Yamhīti yasmiṃ
  anupādisesanibbāne. Bhavānīti liṅgavipallāsena vuttaṃ. Upapattibhavā
  sabbaso anavasesā nirujjhanti nappavattanti. Teti te evaṃ
  vimuttacittā. Dhammasārādhigamāti vimuttisārattā imassa dhammavinayassa
-------------------------------------------------
[page 421]
  Dhammesu sārabhūtassa arahattassa adhigamatoti. Khayeti rāgādikkhayabhūte
  nibbāne ratā abhiratā. Athavā niccabhāvato seṭṭhabhāvato ca
  sabhāvadhammesu sāranti dhammasāraṃ nibbānaṃ. Vuttañcetaṃ virāgo
  seṭṭho dhammānaṃ virāgo tesaṃ aggamakkhāyatīti ca. Tassa
  dhammasārassa adhigamahetu khaye sabbasaṅkhāraparikkhaye anupādisesanibbāne
  ratā. Pahaṃsūti pajahiṃsu. Voti nipātamattanti itivuttakavaṇṇanā. 1-
      [543] Evametaṃ pariyāyena duvidhaṃ hoti.
  Rathavinītasuttavaṇṇanāyampi 2- anupādāparinibbānaṃ nāma appaccayaparinibbānaṃ.
  Dve vā 3- upādānāni gahaṇupādānañca paccayupādānañca.
  Gahaṇupādānaṃ nāma kāmupādānādikaṃ catubbidhaṃ. Paccayupādānaṃ
  nāma avijjāpaccayā saṅkhārāti evaṃ vuttā paccayā. Tattha
  gahaṇupādānavādino ācariyā anupādāparinibbānanti catūsu
  upādānesu aññatarenāpi kañci dhammaṃ aggahetvāva pavattaṃ
  arahattaphalaṃ upādāparinibbānanti 4- kathenti. Tañhi na ca
  upādānasampayuttaṃ hutvā kañci dhammaṃ upādiyati kilesānañca
  parinibbutante jātattā parinibbānanti vuccati. Paccayupādānavādino
  pana paccayavasena anuppannaṃ asaṅkhataṃ amatadhātumeva anupādāparinibbānanti
  kathentīti vuttaṃ.

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. pa. dī. iti. 217. 2. pa. sū. 2/209. 3. dvedhā.
  4. anupādāparinibbānantipi aṭṭhakathāpāṭho.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 422]
      [544] Appaccayaparinibbānanti anupādisesaparinibbānamāha.
  Idāni pakārantarenāpi anupādāparinibbānaṃ dassetuṃ dve vātiādi
  vuttaṃ. Tattha gahaṇupādānanti daḷhaggahaṇabhūtaṃ upādānaṃ. Tenāha
  kāmupādānādikanti. Paccayupādānanti yaṅkañci paccayamāha.
  So hi attano phalaṃ upādiyati upādānavasena gaṇhatīti upādānanti
  vuccati. Tenāha paccayupādānaṃ nāma ... Paccayāti. Anupādāya
  āsavehi cittaṃ vimuccatīti vacanato arahattaphalaṃ anupādāparinibbānanti.
  Na ca upādānasampayuttanti upādānehi sahitaṃ ekasmiṃ patitavasenāpi
  upādānehi saha pavattaṃ hutvā na ca kañci dhammaṃ upādiyatīti kassaci
  dhammassa ārammaṇakaraṇavasena upādiyatīti. Parinibbutanteti
  aggamaggena kātabbakkilesaparinibbānapariyosāne jātattāti taṭṭīkā.
      [545] Idhāpi arahattaphalameva nibbānaṃ. Tenaṭṭhakathāyaṃ idha
  arahattaphalaṃ nibbānanti adhippetaṃ. Tampi hi pañcagativānena
  vānasaññitāya taṇhāya nikkhantattā nibbānanti vuccati. Itarassa
  pana nibbānassa ariyasaccānaṃ dassaneneva sacchikiriyā siddhā tenetaṃ
  idha anadhippetanti vuttaṃ.
      Tattha pañcagativānenāti pañcannaṃ gatīnaṃ vinanato.
      [546] Abhidhammatthasaṅgahaṭīkāyantu bhavābhavaṃ vinanato saṃsibbanato
  vānasaṅkhātāya taṇhāya nikkhantaṃ nibbāti vā etena
  rāgaggiādikoti nibbānanti vuttaṃ.
      Bhavābhavanti duggatisugativasena hīnappaṇītavasena ca khuddakaṃ
-------------------------------------------------
[page 423]
  Mahantañca bhavaṃ. Vuḍḍhatthopi hi akāro dissati asekhā
  dhammātiādīsu viya. Tasmā abhavoti mahābhavo vuccati. Vinanatoti
  etena bhavābhavaṃ vināti heṭṭhūpariyavasena saṃsibbatīti vānanti atthaṃ
  dasseti. Nikkhantanti visayātikkamanavasena atītaṃ. Vānanīti vattabbe
  tadanuparodhenāti paribhāsato vaṇṇāgamo vaṇṇapariyāyoti
  vuttaniruttinayena pubbāparānaṃ vipariyāyavasena nibbānanti vuttaṃ.
  Nibbātīti vūpasamati accantanirodhena nirujjhati. Etenāti
  padena. Ādisaddena dosamohajātijarāmaraṇaggiādayo aggayo
  saṅgaṇhāti. Ettha ca paṭhamavikappe vi saṃsibbaneti dhātu
  vānasaddo kattusādhano nibbānasaddo ca pañcamītappurisasamāso.
  Dutiyavikappe vā gatibandhanesūti 1- vuttepi anekatthā hi dhātavoti
  vuttattā nipubbā vā vūpasameti dhātu nibbānasaddo ca
  karaṇasādhanoti viseso veditabboti maṇisāramañjusā.
      [547] Rāgakkhayādittayañhi arahattassāpi nāmaṃ. Tatrāyaṃ
  saḷāyatane jambukhādakasaṃyuttapāli 2- ekamantaṃ nisinno kho jambukhādako
  paribbājako āyasmantaṃ sārīputtaṃ etadavoca nibbānaṃ nibbānanti
  āvuso sārīputta vuccati katamaṃ nu kho āvuso sārīputta
  nibbānanti. Yo kho āvuso rāgakkhayo dosakkhayo mohakkhayo
  idaṃ vuccati nibbānanti.
                      Nibbānasuttapāli.

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. vā gati-ganthanesūti dhātu dissati. apica vā gati-gandhanesūti dhātupi dissati.
  tampana vā gati-gandhanayo: iti sakkatabhāsānulomena vuttaṃ. 2. saṃ. saḷā. 18/310.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 424]
      Jambukhādakasuttantipi etasseva nāmaṃ.
      [548] Arahattaṃ arahattanti āvuso sārīputta vuccati katamaṃ
  nu kho āvuso sārīputta arahattanti. Yo kho āvuso rāgakkhayo
  dosakkhayo mohakkhayo idaṃ vuccati arahattanti.
                    Arahattasuttapāli. 1-
      [549] Jambukhādako paribbājakoti evannāmo therassa
  bhāgineyyo channaparibbājako. Rāgakkhayoti nibbānaṃ āgamma
  rāgo khīyati tasmā nibbānaṃ rāgakkhayoti vuccati. Dosamohakkhayesupi
  eseva nayo. Yo pana imināva suttena kilesakkhayamattaṃ
  nibbānanti vadeyya so vattabbo kassa kilesānaṃ khayo
  kiṃ attano udāhu paresanti. Addhā attanoti vakkhati.
  Tato pucchitabbo gotrabhūñāṇassa kimārammaṇanti. Jānamāno
  nibbānanti vakkhati. Kimpana gottarabhūñāṇakkhaṇe kilesā khīṇā
  khīyanti khīyissantīti. Khīṇāti vā khīyantīti vā na vattabbā
  khīyissantīti pana vattabbāti. Kimpana tesu akkhīṇesuyeva kilesesu
  gotrabhūñāṇaṃ kilesakkhayaṃ ārammaṇaṃ karotīti. Addhā evaṃ vutte
  niruttaro bhavissati. Maggaññāṇenāpi cetaṃ yojetabbaṃ.
  Maggakkhaṇepi hi kilesā na khīṇā khīyissantīti ca 2- vuccanti na
  ca akkhīṇesuyeva kilesesu kilesakkhayo ārammaṇaṃ hoti. Tasmā
  sampaṭicchitabbametaṃ yaṃ āgamma rāgādayo khīyanti taṃ nibbānanti.

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. saṃ. saḷā. 18/310. 2. khīyantīti panātipi pāṭho. Sī. khīyissantīti ca na.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 425]
  Tampanetaṃ rūpino dhammā arūpino dhammātiādīsu dukesu arūpino
  dhammāti saṅgahitattā na kilesakkhayamattamevāti jambukhādakasuttavaṇṇanā. 1-
      [550] Nibbānaṃ āgammāti ettha āgammāti sabbasaṅkhāresu
  nibbindantassa visaṅkhāraninnassa gotrabhunā vivaṭṭitamānasassa
  maggena sacchikaraṇenāti attho. Sacchikiriyamānañhi taṃ adhigantvā
  ārammaṇappaccayabhūtaṃ paṭicca adhipatippaccayabhūteyeva 2- tasmiṃ paramassāsabhāvena
  vinimuttasaṅkhārassa gatibhāvena 3- ca patiṭṭhābhūte 4- patiṭṭhāya
  khayasaṅkhāto maggo rāgādike khepetīti taṃsacchikaraṇābhāve rāgādīnaṃ
  anuppattinirodhagamanābhāvato taṃ āgamma rāgo khiyyatīti vuttanti
  taṭṭīkā āyatanavibhaṅgaṭīkā ca.
      [551] Visaṅkhāraninnassāti nibbānapoṇassa.
  Vinimuttasaṅkhārassāti samucchedappaṭippassaddhivimuttīhi sammukhena
  tappaṭibaddhacchandarāgappahānena ca suṭṭhu nimuttasaṅkhārassa paramassāsabhāvena
  gatibhāvena ca patiṭṭhābhūte nibbānaṃ arahato gatīti hi vuttaṃ. 5-
  Ṭhitibhāvenātipi pāṭho. Taṃsacchikaraṇābhāveti tassa nibbānassa
  sacchikaraṇābhāveti anuṭīkā.
      [552] Imināva suttenāti imināva jambukhādakasuttena
  kilesakkhayamattaṃ nibbānanti vadeyya rāgakkhayotiādinā sutte

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. sā. pa. 3/162. 2. cevātipi pāṭho. 3. gatibhāvetipi. 4. Sī. ayaṃ
  saddo natthi. 5. vi. parivāra. 8/370.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 426]
  Āgatattā. Kilesakkhayamattanti avisesena vuttattā āha kassāti.
  Addhā attanoti vakkhati parassa kilesakkhayena parassa nibbānappatti
  na yuttāti. Nibbānārammaṇakaraṇena gotrabhūkhaṇe kilesakkhayappatti 1-
  tāva āpannāti āha kimpana tesu akkhīṇesuyevātiādiṃ.
  Nanu ārammaṇakaraṇamattena kilesakkhayo anuppattoti na sakkā
  vattuṃ cittañhi atītānāgatādiṃ sabbaṃ ālambeti na nipphannamevāti.
  Gotrabhūpi maggena yā kilesānaṃ anuppattidhammatā
  kātabbā na taṃ ārabbha pavattissati appattanibbānassa
  nibbānārammaṇaññāṇābhāvato. Na hi aññe dhammā viya nibbānaṃ.
  Tampana atigambhīrattā appattena ālambituṃ na sakkā. Tasmā
  tena gotrabhunā pattabbena tikālikasabhāvātikkantagambhīrabhāvena
  bhavitabbaṃ kilesakkhayamattaṃ vā icchato gotrabhuto puretaruppannena
  kilesakkhayena bhavitabbaṃ. Tenāha akkhīṇesuyeva kilesesu gotrabhūñāṇaṃ
  kilesakkhayaṃ ārammaṇaṃ karotīti. Appattakkilesakkhayārammaṇakaraṇe
  hi sati gotrabhuto puretaraṃ cittānipi ārambeyyunti. Tasmātiādi
  vuttasseva atthassa nigamanaṃ. Tampanetaṃ nibbānaṃ. Arūpino
  dhammātiādīsūti ādisaddena lokuttarānāsavādīnaṃ saṅgaho daṭṭhabbo.
  Arūpino dhammādibhāvaggahaṇena 2- cassa parinipphannatā dīpitā. Tenāha
  na kilesakkhayamattamevāti. Kilesakkhayamattatāya hi nibbānassa bahutā

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. kilesakkhayappavattītipi pāṭho. 2. arūpino dhammādibhāvaggahaṇenāti sabbattha
  īdiso pāṭho dissati. pamādalekhena likhitaṃ maññe. Sī. arūpino dhammāti bhāvaggahaṇena.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 427]
  Āpajjati yattakā kilesā khīyanti tattakāni nibbānānīti
  agādhato gambhīrādibhāvo asaṅkhatādibhāvo ca na siyā. Vutto ca
  so nibbānassa. Tasmāssa paccetabbo parinibbānabhāvo. Yasmā
  ca dhammārammaṇasaṅkhātassa dhammārammaṇaṃ ñāṇaṃ samucchedavasena
  kilese pajahatīti evaṃ parinipphannasaṅkhātaṃ sabhāvantaṃ nibbānanti
  niṭṭhamettha gantabbanti jambukhādakasuttaṭīkā.
      [553] Bahunibbānakathā pana āyatanavibhaṅgavaṇṇanāyaṃ 1- vitthārato
  āgatā.
      Yasmā arahattaṃ rāgadosamohānaṃ khīṇante uppajjati tasmā
  rāgakkhayo dosakkhayo mohakkhayoti vuttanti arahattasuttavaṇṇanā 2-.
      Evaṃ rāgakkhayādittayaṃ arahattassāpi nāmaṃ hoti tasmā
  yathetaṃ tathā nibbānanti idampi arahattassa nāmanti veditabbaṃ.
  Yathāvuttassa nibbānassa sacchikiriyā nāma tassa patti vā paccavekkhaṇā
  vā. Sā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārahetuto maṅgalanti veditabbā. Ettha
  ca diṭṭhadhammoti paccakkho attabhāvo vuccati tattha sukhavihārabhūtā
  arahattaphalasamāpatti diṭṭhadhammasukhavihāro tassa adhigamahetuto
  arahattappatti maṅgalanti.
      [554] Evamimāya gāthāya tapo brahmacariyaṃ ariyasaccadassanaṃ
  nibbānasacchikiriyāti maṅgalacatukkaṃ vuttaṃ. Idha panādo pādattayaṃ
  navanavakkharikaṃ. 3- Brahmacariyasaddo hi paccattekavacanayutto binduntova 4-

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. sam. vi. 66. 2. sā. pa. 3/162.
  3. navakkharikantipi pāṭho. 4. Sī. bindvanto.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 428]
  Dissati kinte vataṃ kimpana brahmacariyaṃ taṃ me vataṃ tampana
  brahmacariyaṃ bhagavati no āvuso brahmacariyaṃ vussatīti ca.
  Tasmā tapo ca brahmacariyañcāti pāṭhova yujjati. Ādhunikā
  pana brahmacariyāti paṭhanti. Tadayuttaṃ. Cakkhu anattā rūpā
  anattāti vattabbe ekenāpi rūpa iti naṭṭhākāraṃ pāṭhaṃ disvā
  tatthato avīmaṃsitvāva rūpaṃ aniccanti mukhāruḷhavasena binduṃ
  pakkhipitvā rūpaṃ anattāti vuttaṃ. Tadanubhāsantā ca tatheva paṭhanti.
  Tampi ayuttaṃ. Evamidhāpi ekena brahmacariya iti naṭṭhabinduṃ pāṭhaṃ
  disvā avīmaṃsitvā dānañca dhammacariyā cāti mukhāruḷhavasena ākāraṃ
  pakkhipitvā brahmacariyāti vuttamaññe tadanubhāsantā ca tatheva
  paṭhanti tasmā brahmacariyāti pāṭho ayutto pālyaṭṭhakathāhi
  virujjhanatoti.
                    Nibbānasacchikiriyākathā.
                    Navamagāthāyatthavaṇṇanā.
                    -----------------
-------------------------------------------------
[page 429]
                     Dasamagāthāyatthavaṇṇanā
      [555] Dasamagāthāyaṃ. Phuṭṭhassāti phusitassa chupitassa sampattassa
  ajjhotthaṭassa. Lokadhammehīti lābhālābhādīhi aṭṭhahi lokadhammehi.
  Phuṭṭhasadde kattā. Na kampatīti na calati na vedhati. Asokanti
  nissokaṃ abbuḷhasokasallaṃ. Virajanti vigatarajaṃ viddhatarajaṃ. Khemanti
  abhayaṃ nirupaddavaṃ. Tatrāyaṃ yojanā yassa lokadhammehi phuṭṭhassa
  puggalassa cittaṃ na kampati tassa etaṃ cittaṃ uttamaṃ maṅgalaṃ
  yassa cittaṃ asokaṃ hoti yassa cittaṃ virajaṃ hoti yassa cittaṃ
  khemaṃ hoti tassa etaṃ cittaṃ uttamaṃ maṅgalanti gaṇhāhi
  devateti. Apica yathā idha brāhmaṇa yassa te honti mātā
  vā pitā 1- vāti ettha yassāti ye assāti chedo evamidhāpi
  yassāti yaṃ assāti chedo yujjati. Assa lokadhammehi phuṭṭhassa
  yañca cittaṃ na kampati yañcassa cittaṃ asokaṃ yañcassa cittaṃ
  virajaṃ yañcassa cittaṃ khemaṃ etaṃ catubbidhaṃ cittaṃ uttamaṃ
  maṅgalanti ayamettha saṅkhepo. Ayantvettha vitthāro.
                       Akampitacittakathā
      [556] Lokadhammā nāma lābhālābhādayo aṭṭha dhammā.
  Tenāha aṭṭhakaṅguttare paṭhamapaṇṇāsakassa paṭhamavagge aṭṭhime
  bhikkhave lokadhammā lokaṃ anuparivattanti anubandhanti 2- loko

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. aṅ. sattaka. 23/46. 2. ayaṃ pāṭho pāliyañceva sīhalapotthake ca na dissati.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 430]
  Ca aṭṭha lokadhamme anuparivattati. Katame aṭṭha. Lābho ca
  alābho ca yaso ca ayaso ca nindā ca pasaṃsā ca sukhañca dukkhañca
  ime kho bhikkhave aṭṭha lokadhammā ... Anuparivattatīti.
                Paṭhame lokadhammasutte 1- pāli.
      [557] Lokassa dhammāti lokadhammā. Etehi vimutto nāma
  natthi buddhānampi honti. Tenevāha lokaṃ anuparivattantīti.
  Anubandhantīti na pajahanti lokato na nivattantīti attho.
  Loko ca aṭṭha lokadhamme anuparivattatīti ayañca loko ete
  anubandhati na pajahati tehi dhammehi na nivattatīti attho. Lābho
  alābhoti lābhe āgate alābho āgatoyevāti veditabbo. Yasādīsupi
  eseva nayoti tabbaṇṇanā 2- saṅgītisutte aṭṭhakavaṇṇanā 3- ca.
      [558] Lokassa dhammāti sattalokassa avassambhāvidhammā.
  Tenāha etehi vimutto nāma natthītiādiṃ. Ghāsacchādanādīnaṃ
  laddhi lābho. Tāni eva vā laddhabbato lābho. Tadabhāvo
  alābho. Lābhaggahaṇena cettha tabbisayo anurodho gahito
  alābhaggahaṇena virodho. Yasmā lohite sati tadupaghātavasena
  pubbo viya anurodhe sati virodho laddhāvasaroeva hoti tasmā
  vuttaṃ lābhe āgate alābho āgatoyevāti. Yasādīsupi eseva
  nayoti taṭṭīkā.

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. aṅ. aṭṭhaka. 23/158. 2. mano. pū. 3/241. 3. su. vi. 3/309.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 431]
      Loke dhammā lokadhammā. Yāva lokappavatti tāva
  anivattakā dhammāti vuttaṃ hotīti idha ettakameva vuttaṃ.
      [559] Cittaṃ nāma mano. Tena curādigaṇe
      cittaṃ mano mānasaṃ viññāṇaṃ hadayaṃ manaṃ
      nāmānetāni vohāra- pathe vattanti pāyatoti vuttaṃ.
      Cintetīti cittaṃ ārammaṇaṃ vijānātīti atthoti
  abhidhammatthasaṅgahaṭīkā 1-.
      Cintadhātu cintāyaṃ vattati. Sā ca cintā tividhā hoti
  uhanacintā vijānanacintā pajānanacintāti. Tāsu uhanacintā
  vitakkassa labbhati vijānanacintā viññāṇassa pajānanacintā
  paññāya. Idha pana cittassa viññāṇattā vijānanacintāva
  labbhatīti taṃ dassento ārammaṇaṃ vijānātīti atthoti āha.
  Cintetīti imassa attho athavā saddo vijānātīti ayaṃ. Athavā
  cintetīti imassa vijānātīti ettha vijānanakiriyāti
  maṇisāramañjusā 2-.
      [560] Cinta cintāyaṃ ārammaṇaṃ cintetīti cittaṃ vijānātīti
  attho. Sabbacittasādhāraṇavasena cetaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ. Ettha siyā
  kasmā ārammaṇaṃ cintetīti cittanti vatvāpi vijānātīti atthoti
  vuttaṃ nanu cintā navijānanasabhāvā 3- na hi cintetīti padassa

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. abhi. vibhāvinī. 66. 2. Ma. mañ. marammapotthake. 1/91.
  3. cintanavijānanasabhāvātipi. Sī. nanu cintanavijānanā nānāsabhāvā.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 432]
  Vijānātīti attho sambhavati duppaññassa hi nānappakārehi
  cintayatopi sukhumatthādhigamo na hotīti. Saccaṃ vijānātīti idaṃ
  padaṃ cittassa saññāpaññākiccavisiṭṭhavisayaggahaṇaṃ dīpetuṃ vuttaṃ
  sabbacittasādhāraṇattā cittasaddassa. Yañhi dhammajātaṃ cittanti
  vuccati tadeva viññāṇaṃ tasmā vijānanatthaṃ gahetvā
  saññāpaññākiccavisiṭṭhavisayaggahaṇaṃ dīpetuṃ vijānātīti vuttanti curādigaṇo.
      Vijānātīti saññāpaññākiccavisiṭṭhavisayaggahaṇamāhāti
  dhammasaṅgaṇīṭīkā.
      Athavā ārammaṇaggahaṇaṃ tividhaṃ sañjānanaṃ vijānanaṃ
  pajānanañcāti. Tesu paṭhamaṃ saññāya kiccaṃ dutiyaṃ viññāṇassa
  tatiyaṃ paññāya. Idha pana cittassa viññāṇattā dutiyameva
  labbhatīti cittassa saññātīraṇappaṭivijjhanasaṅkhātehi saññāpaññākiccehi
  visiṭṭhaṃ ārammaṇūpaladdhisaṅkhātaṃ ārammaṇaggahaṇaṃ dassetuṃ
  ārammaṇaṃ vijānātīti atthoti vuttanti maṇisāramañjusā 1-.
      [561] Sabbesu yaṃ cittesu lokiyakusalākusalamahākiriyācittaṃ
  taṃ javanavīthivasena attano santānaṃ cinotīti cittaṃ. Vipākampana
  kammakkilesehi citanti cittaṃ. Idaṃ cidhātuvasena nibbacanaṃ.
  Yaṅkiñci loke vicittaṃ sippajātaṃ sabbassa tassa citteneva
  karaṇato citteti vicitteti vicittaṃ kayirati etenāti cittaṃ.
  Cittakaraṇatāya cittanti vuttaṃ hoti. Idaṃ cittadhātuvasena

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. Ma. mañ. marammapotthake. 1/91.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 433]
  Nibbacanaṃ. Cittatāya cittaṃ. Idaṃ pāṭipadikavasena nibbacanaṃ. Ettha
  hi cittassa sarāgasadosādibhedabhinnattā sampayuttabhūmiārammaṇahīna-
  majjhimappaṇītādhipativasena cittatā veditabbā. Kiñcāpi ekassa
  cittasaddassa evaṃ vicitratā natthi tathāpi vicitrānaṃ antogadhattā
  samudāyavohārena avayavopi cittanti vuccati yathā
  pabbatanadīsamuddādiekadesesu diṭṭhesu pabbatādayo diṭṭhāti vuccanti.
  Yasmā yattha yattha yathā yathā attho labbhati tattha tattha tathā
  tathā gahetabbo tasmā yaṃ āsevanappaccayabhāvena cinoti yañca
  kammunā abhisaṅkhatattā cittaṃ taṃ tenākārena cittanti vuttaṃ.
  Yampana tathā na hoti taṃ parittakiriyādvayaṃ antimajavanañca
  labbhamānacintanavicittatādivasena cittanti veditabbaṃ. Hasituppādo
  pana aññajavanagatikoyevāti curādigaṇo.
      [562] Cittanti mano mānasanti idha 1- vuttaṃ.
      Mānasanti rāgopi cittampi arahattampi. Antalikkhacaro
  pāso yvāyaṃ carati mānasoti 2- ettha hi rāgo mānasaṃ. Cittaṃ
  mano mānasanti ettha cittaṃ. Appattamānaso sekho kālaṃ
  kayirā janesutāti 3- ettha arahattanti mūlapariyāyasuttavaṇṇanā 4-.
      Taṃsampayuttatāya manasi bhavoti rāgo mānaso. Mano eva
  mānasanti katvā cittaṃ mānasaṃ. Anavasesato mānaṃ sīyati 5- samucchindatīti

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. pa. jo. khu. khu. 168. 2. saṃ. sa. 15/164. 3. saṃ. sa. 15/177.
  4. pa. sū. 1/55. 5. Sī. sayati.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 434]
  Aggamaggo mānasaṃ. Tannibbattattā pana arahattassa mānasatā
  daṭṭhabbā. Janesutāti sattaloke vissutā 1- patthaṭayasāti taṭṭīkā.
      [563] Yassāti navassa vā majjhimassa vā therassa vāti
  idha 2- vuttaṃ.
      Vayovuḍḍhādīsu yo vā so vā hotu paripuṇṇadasavassatāya
  thero ūnapañcavassatāya navo atirekapañcavassatāya majjhimoti
  paṭhamapārājikavaṇṇanā 3-.
      Upasampadāya pañcavassikakālato heṭṭhā jātiyā saṭṭhivassopi
  navoyeva. Pañcavassakālato paṭṭhāya yāva navavassakālā majjhimo
  nāma. Dasavassakālato paṭṭhāya thero nāmāti tikaṅguttare
  dutiyapaṇṇāsakassa pañcamavagge aṭṭhamasuttaṭīkā.
      Aparipuṇṇapañcavasso bhikkhu navo nāma hoti pañcavassato
  paṭṭhāya majjhimo dasavassato paṭṭhāya thero. Aparo nayo.
  Aparipuṇṇadasavasso navo dasavassato paṭṭhāya majjhimo
  vīsativassato paṭṭhāya theroti devatāsaṃyuttassa dutiyavagge
  dasamasuttavaṇṇanā 4-.
      Bhikkhunovādakalakkhaṇampattaṃ theraṃ sandhāya vīsativassato paṭṭhāya
  theroti vuttanti taṭṭīkā.
      [564] Tattha yassa lokadhammehi phuṭṭhassa cittaṃ na kampati

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. taṭṭīkāyampana jane sakalasattaloke vissuta iti dissati.
  2. pa. jo. khu. khu. 168. 3. samanta. 1/278. 4. sā. pa. 1/53.
-------------------------------------------------
[page 435]
  Tassa cittaṃ kenaci akampanīyaṃ lokuttamabhāvāvahanato maṅgalanti
  veditabbaṃ. Kassa ca etehi phuṭṭhassa cittaṃ na kampatīti. Arahato
  khīṇāsavassa na aññassa kassaci. Tathā hi puthujjanasāmaṇerādayo
  lakuṇṭakabhaddiyattheraṃ disvā sīsakaṇṇanāsāsu gahetvā kiṃ cūḷapitā
  sāsane na ukkaṇṭhito abhiramasīti vadanti. Thero tesaṃ neva kujjhati
  na dussati. Bhikkhū kathaṃ samuṭṭhāpesuṃ sāmaṇerādayo evañca evañca
  viheṭhenti so neva kujjhati na dussatīti. Taṃ sutvā satthā āma
  bhikkhave khīṇāsavā nāma neva kujjhanti na dussanti ghanaselasadisā
  hete acalā akampiyāti vatvā dhammapadassa paṇḍitavagge imaṃ gāthamāha
      selo yathā ekaghano vātena na samīrati
      evaṃ nindāpasaṃsāsu na samiñjanti paṇḍitāti.
      Tattha nindāpasaṃsāsūti kiñcāpi idha dve lokadhammā vuttā
  attho pana aṭṭhannampi vasena viditabbo. Yathā hi ghano asusiro
  selo puratthimādibhedena vātena na samīrati na iñjati na calati
  evaṃ aṭṭhasupi lokadhammesu ajjhottharantesu paṇḍitā na samiñjanti
  paṭighavasena vā anunayavasena vā na calanti na kuppantīti tabbaṇṇanā.
      [565] Yantu aṭṭhakathāyaṃ 2-
       selo yathā ekaghano vātena na samīrati
       evaṃ rūpā rasā saddā gandhā phassā ca kevalā
       iṭṭhā dhammā aniṭṭhā ca nappavedhenti tādino

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. khu. dha. 25/25. 2. pa. jo. khu. khu. 169.

[page 436]
       Ṭhitaṃ cittaṃ vippamuttaṃ vayañcassānupassatoti 1-
  vuttaṃ taṃ chakkaṅguttare dutiyapaṇṇāsakassa paṭhamavagge 2- ceva
  cammakkhandhake 3- ca āyasmatā soṇattherena attano arahattaṃ
  byākarontena satthu santike vuttaṃ.
      Tattha phassāti phoṭṭhabbā. Dhammāti manoviññeyyadhammā.
  Nappavedhentīti cittaṃ na kampenti. Tādinoti tādilakkhaṇappattassa.
  Ṭhitanti patiṭṭhitaṃ āneñjappattanti attho.
  Vippamuttanti vipassanāpaṭipattiyā phalasamāpattivasena vimuttaṃ
  nibbānārammaṇe ca adhimuttaṃ. Vayañcassānupassatoti assa cittassa
  uppādampi vayampi anupassantassa arahatoti tabbaṇṇanānayo.
      Tasmimpana sutte cuṇṇiyapade 4- vayañcassānupassatīti āgataṃ.
  Tabbaṇṇanāyañca 5- vayañcassānupassatīti tassa cesa cittassa
  uppādampi vayampi passatīti vuttaṃ. Tassa ca cittassa esa
  khīṇāsavo bhikkhūti attho.
      [566] Evamaṭṭhakathāyaṃ khīṇāsavasseva cittaṃ na kampatīti
  vuttaṃ. Taṃ ukkaṭṭhavasena vuttanti daṭṭhabbaṃ. Tadaññassāpi hi
  ariyasāvakassa cittaṃ na kampatīti sakkā vattuṃ. Tathā hi bhagavā
  assutavatopi bhikkhave puthujjanassa uppajjati lābhopi alābhopi yasopi
  ayasopi nindāpi pasaṃsāpi sukhampi dukkhampi. Sutavatopi bhikkhave

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. vayañcassānupassatīti pāliyaṃ dissati. Sī. īdisoyeva. 2. aṅ. chakka. 22/423.
  3. vi. mahāvagga. 2/11. 4. aṅ. chakka. 22/423. 5. mano. pū. 3/155.

[page 437]
  Ariyasāvakassa uppajjati lābhopi ... Dukkhampi. Ttara bhikkhave
  ko viseso ko adhippāyaso kinnānākaraṇaṃ sutavato ariyasāvakassa
  assutavatā puthujjanenāti evaṃ kathetukamyatāya pucchitvā evamāha
  assutavato bhikkhave puthujjanassa uppajjati lābho so na iti
  paṭisañcikkhati uppanno kho me ayaṃ lābho so ca kho
  anicco dukkho vipariṇāmadhammoti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti. Uppajjati
  alābho ... Uppajjati yaso ayaso nindā pasaṃsā sukhaṃ
  uppajjati dukkhaṃ so na iti paṭisañcikkhati uppannaṃ kho me
  idaṃ dukkhaṃ tañca kho aniccaṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammanti yathābhūtaṃ
  nappajānāti. Tassa lābhopi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati. Alābhopi
  ... Dukkhampi cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati. So uppannaṃ lābhaṃ
  anurujjhati alābhe paṭivirujjhati ... Uppannaṃ sukhaṃ anurujjhati
  dukkhe paṭivirujjhati. So evaṃ anurodhavirodhasamāpanno na parimuccati
  jātiyā jarāmaraṇena sokehi paridevehi dukkhehi domanassehi
  upāyāsehi. Na parimuccati dukkhasmāti vadāmi. Ayaṃ kho bhikkhave
  viseso ayaṃ adhippāyaso idaṃ nānākaraṇaṃ sutavato ariyasāvakassa
  assutavatā puthujjanenāti.
      [567] Lābho alābho ayaso yaso ca 1-
            nindā pasaṃsā ca sukhañca dukkhaṃ 2-
            ete aniccā manujesu dhammā

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. cayasāyaso cāti pāliyaṃ dissati. 2. ca sukhaṃ dukkhañcāti.

[page 438]
            Asassatā vīpariṇāmadhammā
            ete ca ñatvā satimā sumedho
            avekkhatī vīpariṇāmadhamme
            iṭṭhassa dhammā na mathenti cittaṃ
            aniṭṭhato no paṭighātameti
            tassānurodhā athavā virodhā
            vidhūpitā aṭṭhagatā na santi
            padañca ñatvā virajaṃ asokaṃ
            sammappajānāti bhavassa pāragūti 1-.
  Aṭṭhakaṅguttare paṭhamapaṇṇāsakassa paṭhamavagge dutiyalokadhammasutte
                          pāli.
      [568] Ko visesoti kiṃ visesakāraṇaṃ. Adhippāyasoti adhikappayogo.
  Pariyādāyāti gahetvā pariniṭṭhāpetvāti tabbaṇṇanā 2-.
      Adhikaṃ pāyasati payujjati etenāti adhippāyaso savisesaṃ
  ati kattabbakiriyā. Tenāha adhikappayogoti taṭṭīkā.
      Adhikaṃ savisesaṃ pāyasati payujjati etenāti adhippāyaso
  visiṭṭhappayogo. Tenāha adhikappayogoti khandhakavagge
  majjhimapaṇṇāsakaṭīkā.
      Avekkhatī vīpariṇāmadhammeti vipariṇāmadhammā imeti evaṃ
  avekkhati. Vidhūpitāti vidhamitā viddhaṃsitā. Padañca ñatvāti

                       Asokacittakathā
      [569] Soko nāma byasanādihetukaṃ domanassaṃ. Vuttañhi
  mahāsatipaṭṭhānasutte 1- katamo ca bhikkhave soko. Yo kho bhikkhave
  aññataraññatarena byasanena samannāgatassa aññataraññatarena
  dukkhadhammena phuṭṭhassa soko socanā socitattaṃ antosoko
  antoparisoko ayaṃ vuccati bhikkhave sokoti.
      [570] Tattha byasanenāti ñātibyasanādīsu yena kenaci.
  Dukkhadhammenāti vadhabandhādinā dukkhakāraṇena. Phuṭṭhassāti
  ajjhotthaṭassa abhibhūtassa. Sokoti yo ñātibyasanādīsu vā
  vadhabandhādīsu vā aññatarasmiṃ sati tena abhibhūtassa uppajjati
  socanalakkhaṇo soko. Socitattanti socitabhāvo. Yasmā
  panesa abbhantaraṃ sosento parisosento uppajjati tasmā
  antosoko antoparisokoti vuccatīti tabbaṇṇanā 2-.
      Byasanenāti anatthena. Dhammasaddo hetupariyāyoti āha
  dukkhakāraṇenāti. Socananti lakkhitabbatāya socanalakkhaṇo
  socitassa socanakassa puggalassa cittassa vā bhāvo socitabhāvo.
  Abbhantaranti attabhāvassa anto attano lūkhabhāvatāya sosento
  thāmagamanena samantato sosanavasena parisosentoti taṭṭīkā.
      Aniṭṭhārammaṇānubhavanalakkhaṇaṃ domanassaṃ

  *  *  *  *  *  *
  Footnote: 1. dī. mahā. 10/341. 2. su. vi. 2/529.

  Yathātathāaniṭṭhākārasambhogarasaṃ cetasikābādhappaccupaṭṭhānanti
  aṭṭhasālinī 1-.
      [571] So panāyaṃ kiñcāpi atthato domanassavedanā hoti evaṃ
  santepi antonijjhānalakkhaṇo cetaso parinijjhānaraso
  anusocanappaccupaṭṭhānoti saccavibhaṅgavaṇṇanā 2-.
      Pubbe vuttalakkhaṇādikā domanassavedanā ca 3- sokoti puna
  lakkhaṇādayo na vattabbā siyuṃ tathāpi domanassavisesattā
  sokabhāvavisiṭṭhā lakkhaṇādayo vattabbāti kiñcāpītiādimāha.
  Visāradarahitamanto 4- evaṃ saṅkupitacintanaṃ sukkhanaṃ vā antonijjhānaṃ
  dahanaṃ ñātibyasanādianurūpaṃ socanaṃ anusocanaṃ. Taṃ taṃ vā guṇaṃ
  dosañca anugantvā socanaṃ tappanaṃ anusocananti taṭṭīkā.
      Saṅkupitacintananti pītisomanassappaṭipakkhato dosasampayogato
  ca ārammaṇe anabhiratippavattimāha. Anto attano nissayassa
  niddahanavasena vā jhānaṃ cintanaṃ antonijjhānaṃ. Sati anusocanabhāve
  attano katākatakusalākusalavisayo manovilekhabhūto vippaṭisāro kukkuccaṃ
  yathāvuttaṃ antonijjhānaṃ sokoti ubhinnaṃ viseso veditabboti anuṭīkā.
      [572] Tasmā dukkhadhammena phuṭṭhenapi na socitabbaṃ atīte
  pañcajanādīhi viya.
      Atīte bārāṇasiyaṃ bodhisatto brāhmaṇo hutvā kasikammena
  jīvitaṃ 5- kappesi. Tassa putto dhītā cāti dve puttā. So

  Vayappattassa puttassa samānakulato kumārikaṃ ānesi. Iti te
  pañca janā ekāya khujjuttarāya dāsiyā saddhiṃ cha ahesuṃ.
  Bodhisatto bhariyādīnaṃ ovādaṃ adāsi maraṇassatiṃ bhāvetha tumhākaṃ
  maraṇabhāvaṃ sallakkhetha imesañca sattānaṃ maraṇaṃ dhuvaṃ jīvitaṃ
  addhuvaṃ sabbe saṅkhārā aniccā khayavayadhammino rattiñca divā
  ca appamattā hothāti. Te tassovāde ṭhatvā maraṇassatiṃ
  bhāvesuṃ. Athekadivasaṃ bodhisatto puttena saddhiṃ khettaṃ gantvā
  kasati. Putto kacavaraṃ saṅkaḍḍhitvā jhāpesi. Tassāvidūre ekasmiṃ
  vammike āsīviso atthi. Dhūmo tassa akkhīni pahari. So kuddho
  nikkhamitvā taṃ ḍaṃsi. So maritvāva pati. Bodhisatto tassa
  matabhāvaṃ ñatvā taṃ ukkhipitvā ekasmiṃ rukkhamūle nipajjāpetvā
  arodantova bhijjanadhammaṃ bhinnaṃ maraṇadhammaṃ mataṃ sabbe saṅkhārā
  aniccā maraṇanipphattikāti aniccabhāvameva sallakkhetvā khettaṃ
  kasanto khettasamīpe gacchantaṃ ekaṃ paṭivissakaṃ purisaṃ disvā tāta
  gehaṃ gacchasīti pucchi. Āmāti. Tenahi amhākampi gharaṃ gantvā
  brāhmaṇiṃ vadeyyāsi ajja kira pubbe viya dvinnaṃ bhattaṃ
  anāharitvā ekasseva āhareyyātha pubbe ca ekikā dāsī
  āharati ajja pana cattāropi janā āgaccheyyāthāti. So
  gantvā tatheva kathesi. Te sabbepi tattha gantvā taṃ mataṃ disvā
  gandhapupphehi pūjetvā jhāpesuṃ. Ekassāpi roditaṃ vā paridevitaṃ
  vā nāhosi ekabindumattampi assu nāhosi. Sabbe hete

[page 443]
  Subhāvitamaraṇassatino. Tesaṃ guṇatejena sakkassa bhavanaṃ uṇhaṃ jātaṃ.
  Sakko āvajjento taṃ kāraṇaṃ ñatvā pasannacitto tattha gantvā te
  asocanakāraṇaṃ pucchi. Tepissa kathesuṃ. Tesu hi bodhisatto evamāha
    [573] Uragova tacaṃ jiṇṇaṃ hitvā gacchati santanuṃ
          evaṃ sarīre nibbhoge pete kālākate sati
          ḍayhamāno na jānāti ñātīnaṃ paridevitaṃ.
          Tasmā etaṃ ma socāmi gato so tassa yā gatīti.
      Tattha uragovāti yathā urago jiṇṇaṃ tacaṃ hitvā anapekkho
  hutvā chaḍḍetvā gacchati. Santanunti attano sarīraṃ. Nibbhogeti
  jīvitindriyābhāvena bhogarahite. Peteti paralokaṃ paṭigate.
  Kālakateti katakāle katamaraṇeti attho. Satīti tasmiṃ me
  putte pete maraṇakālaṃ katvā vigate sati ko ruṇṇena vā
  paridevena vā attho. Ḍāayhamānoti yatheva hi ayasūlehi
  vajjhitvā ḍayhamāno sukhadukkhaṃ na jānāti evaṃ ñātīnaṃ paridevanaṃ
  na jānāti. Tasmāti tena kāraṇenāhaṃ etaṃ na socāmi yā
  cassa attano gati taṃ so gatoti.
      Brāhmaṇīpi āha
    [574] Anavhāto tato āgā ananuññāto ito gato
          yathāgato tathā gato tattha kā paridevanā
          ḍayhamāno na jānāti ... Gato so tassa yā gatīti.
      Tattha anavhātoti ayaṃ mayā paralokato anavhāto ayācitova.

[page 444]
  Āgāti amhākaṃ gehaṃ āgato. Itoti manussalokato
  gacchantopi mayā ananuññātova gato. Yathāgatoti āgacchantopi
  yathā attano ruciyā āgato gacchanto tatheva gato. Tatthāti
  tasmiṃ tassa ito gamane kā paridevanā.
      Kaniṭṭhabhaginīpi āha
    [575] Sace rode kisā assaṃ tassā me kiṃ phalaṃ siyā
          ñātimittasuhajjānaṃ bhiyyo no aratī siyā
          ḍayhamāno na jānāti ... Tassa yā gatīti.
      Tattha rodeti yadi ahaṃ jeṭṭhabhātari mate rodeyyaṃ. Kisā
  assanti kisasarīrā bhaveyyaṃ bhātu pana me tappaccayā vuḍḍhi nāma
  natthīti dasseti. Kiṃ phalanti tassā me rodantiyā ko ānisaṃso
  bhaveyya avuḍḍhi pana paññāyatīti dīpeti. Ñātimittasuhajjānanti
  ñātimittasuhadānaṃ. Ayameva pāṭho. Bhiyyo noti ye
  amhākaṃ ñātī ca mittā ca suhajjā ca tesaṃ atirekatarā
  arati siyā.
      Bhariyāpissa āha
    [576] Yathāpi dārako candaṃ gacchantaṃ anurodati
          evaṃ sampadamevetaṃ yo petamanusocati
          ḍayhamāno ... Yā gatīti.
      Tattha dārakoti yuttāyuttaṃ labbhanīyālabbhanīyaṃ ajānanto
  bāladārako mātu ucchaṅge nisinno puṇṇacandaṃ ākāsena gacchantaṃ

[page 445]
  Disvā amma candaṃ me dehi candaṃ me dehīti punappunaṃ rodati.
  Evaṃ sampadamevetanti evaṃ nipphattikameva tassa etaṃ anusocanaṃ
  hoti yo petaṃ kālakataṃ anusocati. Itopica niratthakataraṃ.
  Kiṃkāraṇā. So hi vijjamānaṃ candaṃ anusocati mayhaṃ pana pati
  mato etarahi avijjamānoti.
      Dāsīpi āha
    [577] Yathā brahme udakumbho bhinno appaṭisandhiyo
          evaṃ sampadamevetaṃ yo petamanusocati
          ḍayhamāno ... Yā gatīti.
      Tattha udakumbhoti udakakumbho ukkhipiyamāno patitvā
  sattadhā bhinno yo petaṃ anusocati tassāpi etaṃ anusocanaṃ evaṃ
  nipphattikameva hoti matassa puna jīvāpetuṃ asakkuṇeyyattā. Iddhimatā va
  iddhānubhāvena bhinnaṃ kumbhaṃ sandhahitvā udakassa pūretuṃ sakkā bhaveyya
  kālakato pana iddhibalenāpi na sakkā paṭipākatikaṃ kātunti.
      [578] Sakko sabbesaṃ dhammakathaṃ sutvā pasīditvā ito paṭṭhāya
  sahatthā kammaṃ mā karittha ahaṃ sakko devarājā tumhākaṃ gehe
  aparimāṇasattaratanāni karissāmi tumhe dānaṃ detha sīlaṃ rakkhatha
  uposathaṃ upavasathāti vatvā tathā katvāva sakaṭṭhānameva gato.
      Iti te pañca janā asocanamūlakaṃ aparimitadhanaṃ paṭilabhiṃsu.
      Satthāpi mataputtassa ekassa kuṭumbikassa sokaṃ nīharituṃ imaṃ
  atītavatthuṃ āharitvā saccāni pakāsesi. Saccapariyosāne so
